# User scenarios and test gaps

NixOS machine integration tests and property-based tests are the preferred evidence. Unit tests establish narrower behavior. **Present** means a test exists and its assertion was inspected; **recorded** links a historical execution record, not a rerun of the current tree. Partial coverage does not mean the feature is broken. Gaps identify what the evidence cannot establish.

## Start the installed Codex or Claude Code from a guest terminal or SSH account using private Ziggy inference

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, authorization, transport, execution, isolation, storage, recovery.

Boundary: Native user-run client configuration and guest-local state remain separate from Marshall's platform agent loop; Lilly derives the VM binding and Marshall grants current inference authority. Codex starts with no approvals and danger-full-access; Claude starts with bypassPermissions and no Bash sandbox. Guest OS, VM, network and Marshall boundaries remain independent. Ubuntu retains the pinned offline application closure through its registered GC root.

Required refusal: Dangerous client defaults remove prompts and client-level command sandboxing but cannot manufacture identity, revive stale execution, bypass Customer private-inference refusal or enable a disabled provider. Wrong CA trust or hostname must fail rather than disable TLS verification.

Tested scope: Integrated core gates and exact enrolled-host image graphs passed; the exact Ubuntu rootfs rebuild reproduced bytes in 72 seconds. Both complete client fleets passed in NixOS 352.54 seconds and Ubuntu 432.98 seconds, covering native personal-account PTY inference, separate curl beta counting, limited trust refusal, Customer no-effects refusal, root GC and registered verified SSH inference after GC. Fleet images differ from production artifacts. Marshall/static/Nix handbook and earlier complete NixOS native gates passed at c5747868. Complete corrected Ubuntu passed in 2,203.23 seconds with twelve subtests and final clone/cleanup guard. Focused current NixOS passed the exact 200-line prefix in 1,246.95 seconds, six subtests through original neighbour cleanup; later 161 lines retain earlier full-run scope. Both new actions exercised exact reuse only; device-only replacement remains unexecuted and original failures remain recorded. Corrected/refrozen packaged handbook rendering and reader revocation passed in 47.18 seconds at its separately captured source; this does not add guest or production acceptance. Separately, published 8930a70f activated healthy on both hosts with matching fresh production image offers. Its first NixOS Codex canary timed out before any inference admission and was fully cleaned up; Ubuntu creation was not issued. Pinned public-chain rejection, isolated constrained Codex controls, same-root preparation and independently verified encrypted host replacements are distinct evidence scopes, without commit/push or live replacement claims for the leaf change. The current native settings also pin Codex approval_policy=never and sandbox_mode=danger-full-access plus Claude permissions.defaultMode=bypassPermissions and sandbox.enabled=false; native probes execute ordinary commands without launch flags. This does not establish host or VM isolation from a guest process.

**Gaps:** Both original full Ubuntu attempts failed at fresh neighbour startup with template_quarantined. The 507.23-second and 449.24-second diagnostics stopped after original VM 102 start/write without reproducing failure or completing deletion/survival. Later imported inspection demonstrated st_dev-only strict refusal, not the failure-instant predicate; original cause remains unknown. Both final actions exercised exact reuse only. Device-only public-cache quarantine/retirement/deletion/replacement remains unexecuted. Current NixOS focus covers only the 200-line prefix; its later 161 lines retain prior full-run scope. The first production NixOS Codex timeout retained no child TLS stderr; Ubuntu creation was not issued. Independent old-chain rejection, same-root preparation and encrypted round-trips do not establish commit/push of the leaf change, both-host live Rustls/SSH acceptance or successful renewed production canaries. Limited Codex/curl trust refusals do not establish every Claude mode. Native/fleet fixtures, production artifacts, live providers, sustained load, independent physical hosts and retained-disk upgrades retain separate scopes; production Anthropic remains disabled. Dangerous defaults intentionally remove client-level approval and sandbox protections; guest OS/VM/network/Marshall boundaries remain, and no stronger local confidentiality claim is made.

**Preferred test work:** Retain the unexecuted device-only replacement branch, original unknown template-failure cause, focused/full native scope and failed production canary explicitly. Complete ADR-0111's same-root publication and both-host live Rustls/SSH obligations, then record renewed production canaries separately from activated source/images, offline proof and passing isolated-provider fixtures. Keep the deliberate dangerous-mode policy and its isolation limits explicit in future probes.

## Send native Anthropic Messages or token-counting beta requests through Ziggy

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: authorization, transport, execution, storage, recovery.

Boundary: Ziggy's closed native route type validates the exact beta query separately from the query-free accounting path, forwards native bytes and allowed Anthropic headers, and retains current admission and provider-secret custody.

Required refusal: Reject wrong, duplicate, additional or encoded query parameters, mismatched prefixes, non-POST methods and OpenAI beta queries before provider effects. Disabled Anthropic returns provider_unavailable before authority or spool access.

Tested scope: Pure generated exact-query/refusal and packaged verified-TLS beta URL/native bytes/header/query-free accounting checks retain their recorded scopes. Both complete native-client fleets passed actual Claude Messages and separate curl beta counting through Firecracker/canonical accounts, verified Audi TLS, real Marshall admission and isolated providers, including SSH after GC and Customer refusal. Service control remains synthetic. Captured Marshall/static/Nix handbook, earlier complete NixOS, corrected complete Ubuntu and focused current NixOS prefix passed with exact post-import reuse only.

- **property, recorded**: [only the exact Anthropic beta query reaches a native route](repo:ziggy/test/route_test.exs) — Generated arbitrary query strings can select only literal beta=true on the closed Anthropic Messages/token-counting routes; native/prefixed path and body remain separate from the validated query. Local pure routing does not establish native TLS or provider execution.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-13-native-guest-coding-clis.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [disabled provider rejects before accessing authority or spool](repo:ziggy/test/provider_selection_test.exs) — Every native/prefixed Messages and token-counting path, with empty or beta query, refuses disabled Anthropic before authority/spool dependency access.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-13-native-guest-coding-clis.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [native Anthropic beta URLs preserve request bytes and query-free accounting](repo:nix/tests/machines/ziggy-service/driver.py) — Actual packaged Ziggy and verified-TLS provider fixture preserve Messages/token-counting beta query, native body and allowed headers while recording query-free operation names. Synthetic control admission; no Firecracker or real Claude client.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-13-native-guest-coding-clis.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [unsupported query aliases reject before provider effects](repo:nix/tests/machines/ziggy-service/driver.py) — Malformed, duplicate/additional, OpenAI and mismatched-provider beta URLs leave the finite upstream request inventory unchanged.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-13-native-guest-coding-clis.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [native user terminal loads both clients and streams through Ziggy](repo:nix/tests/machines/fleet/scenarios/coding-clis.py) — Both complete integrated actual-client fleets passed in 352.54/432.98 seconds. Native Claude managed-settings Messages succeeds through real Firecracker/Audi/Marshall authority and isolated provider TLS; separate guest curl beta counting is observed. Terminal inference, SSH after GC and Customer refusal complete. Claude-initiated counting, live providers, editor-bearing production image activation and independent physical hosts remain outside this scope.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-13-native-guest-coding-clis.md).

**Gaps:** Both native-client fleets, corrected complete Ubuntu and focused current NixOS prefix passed. Both final post-import actions exercised exact reuse only; device-only replacement remains unexecuted and both original Ubuntu failures unexplained. Later omitted NixOS cases retain their prior full-run scope. Final pre-publication structural gates passed and 8930a70f activated healthy on both hosts, but the first production NixOS Codex canary timed out without inference admission; Ubuntu creation was not issued. Same-root leaf publication/live verification, renewed production client acceptance, live Anthropic and sustained capacity remain separate. Fleet images omit the unrelated editor and differ from production artifacts. Production Anthropic remains disabled; Claude is not claimed to initiate token counting.

**Preferred test work:** Preserve captured native/client-fleet passes and separate pure-route/synthetic-control scopes; record same-root leaf live verification and renewed production canaries without enabling production Anthropic by implication or claiming device-only replacement execution.

## Open a trusted guest project with immutable AI policy and reviewed default extensions

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: execution, isolation, storage, recovery.

Boundary: The exact source-built package supplies immutable policy and signed hash-pinned Open VSX builtins; native workers use current personal UID and all-folder trust. Installed extensions/server data belong to guest-account directories, while disablement follows upstream browser-profile storage on the separate account origin without cross-device synchronization. Language tools come from project environments and direnv requires separate explicit approval.

Required refusal: Refuse conflicting AI settings, direct Chat/LM dispatch and restored Chat editors. Reject wrong signature/key/hash/manifest/platform/engine/dependency/archive, incomplete registry pagination and observed concurrent lock changes. Prepublication failure preserves the old lock; postrename synchronization failure reports durability uncertainty with complete new bytes potentially visible. Missing project servers fail without bundled installer fallback. Workspace trust never approves an envrc or grants Marshall admission.

Tested scope: Compiled policy, signed extension maintenance and package runtime checks retain their exact historical releases. Startup release 73ad passed its 217s package, 28s installed-validator check and separate fresh-profile restored-workbench smoke. Both corrected pre-template snapshot complete family scripts passed in 805s/829s total build plus execution. Initial standalone and language failures retain their scopes, followed by scoped personal/language successes on older packages. Final release 7d2415 passed its 236s package with full client TypeScript and 344 compiled-helper decisions, 7s installed-validator maintenance and 99s matching guest image builds. A strict scratch check passed 36 actual Workbench preference decisions and bounded forced-install filtering. After run 28874 failed its stderr-capture oracle despite the correct refusal, corrected full standalone run 70655 passed in 342s total build/execution: both-account defaults/direnv, lower/core/System refusals, newer override/reversion, independent personal controls, actual Rust/Elixir and broker/worker recovery. This is native package/broker scope. Production 63a/release 73ad host/image activation is recorded without a browser journey.

- **machine, recorded**: [personal newer versions override immutable defaults and uninstall restores the system version](repo:nix/tests/machines/vscode-web/default.nix) — Complete run 70655 passed final package 7fdh/release 7d2415 with the real broker, trusted HTTPS and personal Linux UIDs: all defaults/direnv, lower/core/System refusals, actual newer install/reversion, personal disable/overrides, real rustc Error E0308 and Elixir responses, marker custody and broker/origin/worker recovery. Immutable c829s4f5 snapshot; total 342s and driver 323.30s. Synthetic authorizer/relay do not establish Marshall/Audi or Firecracker family/production acceptance; historical lower/equal scanner state remains separate.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-10-browser-editor.md).

Installed-validator maintenance passed in 7s and both matching manifest-v3 image builds in 99s. ## Refuse stale guest attribution while retaining genuine native exit evidence

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, execution, isolation, recovery.

Boundary: Volatile proof binds the exact currently verified VMM; one native observer may outlive proof withdrawal to report its actual terminal outcome, without granting authority.

Required refusal: Negative evidence immediately withdraws proof; unavailable or unarmed targets retain the short backstop, stale ports cannot renew it, and a different boot target cannot inherit live authority.

Tested scope: Deterministic real-port ordering regressions, generated watch-machine terminal transitions and the final typed native Brady VMM-kill/attribution-refusal journey pass. Actual exact-target watcher revocation took 99 milliseconds under the original less-than-1000-millisecond bound.

Error, target replacement, unavailable binding, boot-only replacement, stale-port and shutdown cases pass separately. No native pidfd or physical isolation claim.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-09-observability-decoupling.md).
- **property, recorded**: [terminal reports always revoke and only :absent opens a watcher](repo:lilly/test/vm/actor_watch_test.exs) — Generated watcher-machine terminal transitions require revocation and bounded open effects. This property does not generate actor proof-target or journal/port ordering histories and does not establish native process behavior.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-09-observability-decoupling.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [brady-vmm-death-revokes-via-watcher](repo:nix/tests/machines/fleet/scenarios/brady.py) — Real target VMM SIGKILL with one bound watcher and matching pidfd; actual exact-placement exited witness, 99-millisecond revocation under the original subsecond requirement and unresolved old-flow attribution. Complete typed Brady native script passed in 359.64 seconds on the captured pre-Starship guest runtime; no physical host failure claim.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-09-observability-decoupling.md).

**Gaps:** Generated cross-target observation/arming histories remain absent. Native acceptance uses the captured guest runtime before incoming Starship defaults; physical host failure remains separate.

## Hold every Lilly lease and leased utility in lilly-hostd until the holder releases it or dies

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: authorization, execution, storage, recovery.

Boundary: lilly-hostd holds each lease's flock on the existing lock inode, grants waiters in arrival order and runs leased utilities from a closed table with the lease descriptor inherited, each in its own command cgroup in production.

Required refusal: No successor lease while a utility of the previous holder may run; no mutating utility without a lease; no second command on a busy lease; no session for a wrong uid, cgroup or protocol version.

Tested scope: hostd library tests (decoder and lease-order Hegel properties, real-utility session custody) and Lilly lock tests against the real daemon. Unit tests use process-group custody; cgroup custody is exercised only by module-based NixOS machine tests.

- **property, recorded**: [lease_grants_follow_arrival_order](repo:lilly/native/hostd/src/tests.rs) — Generated exclusive and shared waiter sequences behind a held lease; grant order and mutual exclusion replayed against an independent model.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-16-lilly-hostd-core.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [holder-death-kills-leased-command](repo:lilly/test/host/lock_test.exs) — A holder killed with a cp blocked on a FIFO under its lease; the successor is granted only after hostd killed the utility.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-16-lilly-hostd-core.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [completed native launch survives abrupt worker BEAM loss and fresh Store reopen](repo:nix/tests/machines/zfs-guest/actor-recovery.py) — Real guests created, read and deleted through the production lilly-hostd unit with cgroup custody, including abrupt worker BEAM loss.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-16-lilly-hostd-core.md).

**Gaps:** Arguments remain caller-supplied until typed native effects replace the utility table. The peer cgroup check reads /proc after connect rather than through a pidfd. Lilly and hostd both run as root until the privilege drop.

**Preferred test work:** A dedicated host-effects-custody machine test for wrong-peer refusal, BEAM SIGKILL mid-command and escaped descendants under cgroup custody.

## Inspect physical and workflow completion independently of receipt polling

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, authorization, transport, execution, storage, recovery.

Boundary: Compute owns transactional measurement admissions and retained numeric totals; authenticated physical outcomes and exact-execution readiness evidence supply observations without changing execution or receipt authority.

Required refusal: Missing or late physical/readiness evidence cannot succeed; wrong-generation readiness cannot finish creation. Replayed admissions and scans cannot double-count. A measurement timeout must not release capacity, cancel work or replay provider effects.

Tested scope: Generated accounting histories, Ash database admission/replay/rollback and recorded native clone/relocation measurements. Complete cloning-fleet passed unchanged-process repeated Start, clone retry/cancellation measurement with exact replay, occupied-stream cancellation, restoration/cleanup, independent cloned-guest relocations and audit after source deletion.

## Request a magic link and receive a usable sign-in message after an uncertain mail attempt

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, authorization, transport, recovery.

Boundary: The encrypted outbox row controls whether a mail attempt may start; each retry carries one stable Message-ID and token payload, while token consumption remains single-use.

Required refusal: Refuse a new SMTP attempt after the delivery commits delivered or expired; an uncertain earlier provider acceptance may be duplicated with the same identity.

Tested scope: Generated adapter/verified-TLS timeout retries and committed three-peer contention/crash properties exercise locked Ash delivery. Waiting peers independently recheck removed or expired stored tokens after explicit SMTP rejection.

## Create a machine and wait for its exact boot to become usable over SSH

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, authorization, transport, execution, isolation, storage, recovery.

Boundary: A currently registered Porsche key and durable guest account enter the Audi proxy under strict host-CA trust. Host-local provisioning progress cannot grant launch authority; readiness binds the owned placement, epoch, generation and boot. A materialized file is independent of its template.

Required refusal: Reject direct guest SSH bypass, stale boot receipts, deadline extension, corrupt journals, overwritten disks and unconfirmed storage cleanup; retain the original mutation ID through uncertain replies. Exact sealed identity changes refuse reuse; quarantined incarnations cannot regain readiness and require fenced retirement plus full verification of a distinct replacement.

Tested scope: Guarded provisioning journals, Rust/Hegel helper, native ZFS copy and template crash recovery. At the historical reader release, compatible readers and pool_id migration were deployed healthy on both hosts with producers off. Current declarations enable templates and block-cloned copies on both hosts, and every create copies its root disk on demand (ADR-0177 retired the earlier 128 standbys per image); incoming e3300040 reported production activation and samples from the standby configuration without independently captured deployment receipts. Two uncontrolled NixOS/Ubuntu SSH canaries confirmed run-owned cleanup through Audi. Source-inspected recurring template sweep schedules ten-minute retries through unchanged image-lease, pool-lock and native ownership refusals, with catalogued finite failure stages; periodic contention/retry has no newly recorded native or property execution.

Production template producers were off at that historical reader boundary. The final typed focused NixOS Porsche lifecycle variant now passes on its captured pre-Starship images and fixtures; Ubuntu lifecycle and the separate complete clone fault matrix remain unrun. The first live benchmark canary failed direct SSH without Audi and its original deletion outlasted the runner deadline, then was independently confirmed cleaned. Two corrected readers-off production canaries passed client SSH and cleanup, not template-enabled readiness. A readers-off campaign passed ten sequential authenticated SSH samples per distribution but failed two of four concurrent creations with execution_failed; the failed run-owned VMs were independently confirmed deleted with released reservations and absent ZFS datasets using their preplanned deletion IDs. Sequential medians are exploratory, not an accepted matched baseline. The unrun Ubuntu Porsche lifecycle and separate complete clone fault matrix, delayed bootstrap/template-hit acceptance, complete concurrent baseline/enabled benchmark and independently observed current producer rollout remain. Current NixOS lifecycle evidence retains its captured pre-Starship scope. The formerly stuck baseline fixture is confirmed deleted. ADR-0143 offload, compressed same-host clones and deletion under pool writes pass native ZFS tests but lack post-deploy production measurement; compressed local clone throughput on real disks is unmeasured. ADR-0149 retires diagnostic ingestion/outbox/presentation; pre-cutover evidence is historical. Replacement native/property acceptance must execute separately. ADR-0177's two 26-create production bursts (with and without standbys) are recorded in the ADR; no machine test plants a leftover standby-* dataset to show the pool withheld natively.

**Preferred test work:** Storage tests use synthetic artifacts; execute the unrun Ubuntu Porsche lifecycle and separate complete clone fault matrix, plus delayed certificate/roster/SSH readiness, template-hit and stale-authority refusals. Current NixOS lifecycle evidence remains scoped to its captured pre-Starship runtime. The first four-way baseline failed two guarded ZFS observations; the prepared placement-bound stage diagnostic is not deployed and has not identified their precise cause. Before retrying, independently rebuild and compare the complete Ubuntu rootfs, manifest and NAR on both enrolled hosts after the sandboxed guest Nix DB normalization; the existing same-derivation output drift blocks an exact matched image offer. Keep the separate terminal fleet failure recorded until its database/browser cause is resolved. Execute the replacement independent-observability native and property scenarios; historical receipts cannot establish this cutover. Add a real contended image-lease/pool-lock periodic sweep retry that confirms retirement only after ownership is acquired, plus generated first-failure stage histories.

## Redeem one banked Codex reset credit for a chosen host-local account

Coverage: **gap**. Layers: authorization, storage, recovery.

Boundary: The reset route, page button and underclass reset command require the host's admin bearer token; a remote --url call must carry UNDERCLASS_UI_TOKEN and never sends a database token.

Required refusal: Proxy-only, monitor-socket and unauthenticated callers cannot redeem credits; without force the proxy refuses an allowed or already-redeemed account with 409, and a missing credit or non-Codex account is never overridable.

Tested scope: Source review of upstream d0cb274 and an offline fleet package build only; no tests executed for this pin update.



**Gaps:** Authenticated redemption, the 409 refusals and force override, idempotency across restart and live upstream credit consumption have not been verified for this revision.

**Preferred test work:** Extend the Underclass NixOS service test with a controlled credit issuer; drive the reset route and CLI, check refusals, force and restart idempotency; run upstream reset fixtures.

## Inspect bounded Underclass stream outcomes and long-range capacity statistics

Coverage: **gap**. Layers: authorization, transport, storage, recovery.

Boundary: The pinned Underclass proxy stores sanitized stream outcomes and aggregate statistics in its host-local database; the stats API, event import route and CLI require the host administrator bearer token.

Required refusal: Proxy-only, monitor-socket and unauthenticated callers cannot read or import statistics; prompt bodies, cache keys, credentials and unsanitized upstream error text never enter the statistics store.

Tested scope: Source review of upstream 8c1a38c and an offline fleet package build only; no tests executed for this pin update.



**Gaps:** Authenticated statistics queries, event import deduplication, retention pruning, rollup recovery and live provider capacity classification have not been verified for this revision.

**Preferred test work:** Extend the Underclass NixOS service test with controlled streams and an event importer; verify authorization, sanitized persistence, retention, rollups, bounded windows and restart recovery.

## Rotate host-local Codex credentials and recover rejected provider accounts

Coverage: **gap**. Layers: authorization, storage, recovery.

Boundary: Each host owns its independent account database and refresh tokens; account controls require its admin bearer token.

Required refusal: Proxy-only and unauthenticated callers cannot administer accounts; Enable must preserve quota cooldowns, and scheduled refresh failure must preserve account health.

Tested scope: Source review of upstream 3e35c3b only; no tests executed for this pin update.



**Gaps:** Native legacy-database migration, scheduled rotation, issuer failure, authenticated recovery and live provider behavior have not been verified for this revision.

**Preferred test work:** Extend the Underclass NixOS service test with a legacy database and controlled issuer; run upstream generated rotation-interval properties and recovery fixtures. Tests were skipped at the user's request.

## Sign in for ordinary access, verify a passkey for privileged actions, and revoke a session

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, authorization.

Boundary: A current stored login permits ordinary access independently of a passkey. Single-use WebAuthn challenges establish session-specific privileged elevation. Browser magic-link GET leaves the single-use token intact; connected LiveView posts it with remember enabled.

Required refusal: Reject expired/replayed challenges and revoke authority held by stale actors. Invalid or replayed magic links establish neither login nor remembered authority.

Tested scope: Real signed WebAuthn fixtures and generated independent-login histories; native subsequent magic-link and remembered login plus administrator revocation across three colocated serving replicas with a virtual authenticator. Campaign 015 separately checks the packaged standalone browser helper with pinned ty; recorded native execution retains its original source and scope.

- **machine, recorded**: [native-subsequent-mfa-login](repo:nix/tests/machines/mfa-login/browser.py) — Three colocated serving replicas, virtual WebAuthn, subsequent magic-link and remembered login of adjacent sessions, actual backend and WebSocket observations, admin-UI mounted/copied-cookie revocation including a no-MFA account, and an expired-ceremony refusal (SQL-backdated challenge deadline) followed by a fresh verified ceremony served by a different replica.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-login-races.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [magic-link sign-in and account revocation serialize in both orders on three nodes](repo:marshall/test/marshall/compute/distributed_admission_test.exs) — Advisory-gated real sign-in create action versus administrator session revocation, MFA reset and retirement on three independent BEAMs sharing committed PostgreSQL; both orders; issued session and remembered credentials read from committed token rows; ordered-after global revocation remains an intentional fresh sign-in.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-login-races.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [controller sign-out and remembered login exchange serialize in both orders on three nodes](repo:marshall/test/marshall/compute/distributed_admission_test.exs) — Ordinary sign-out of a remember-only browser raced against an in-flight remembered exchange; exchange committed first survives as an independent session while its credential is then revoked; committed sign-out fences a later exchange into refusal.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-login-races.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [a login revoked between issuance and the success callback refuses the session](repo:marshall/test/marshall_web/auth_controller_test.exs) — Revoked-before-callback browser login: clean redirect refusal, no stored session, generic message, closed-reason event without token material.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-login-races.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [challenge issuance prunes expired rows and bounds retained challenges per account](repo:marshall/test/marshall/accounts/security_test.exs) — Retained-row bound of ten per account with the specific :try_later boundary, expired-row pruning on issuance, and refill to the same boundary.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-login-races.md).
- **unit, present**: [delivered link automatically submits a remembered sign-in once](repo:marshall/test/marshall_web/magic_link_flow_test.exs) — HTTP GET preserves the token; sign-in POST issues a stored login and remembered cookie for new, existing and MFA-enabled users; invalid replay is refused. Browser auto-submit is verified separately by the recorded machine test.
- **machine, recorded**: [assert any("remember" in cookie["name"] for cookie in page.context.cookies())](repo:nix/tests/machines/mfa-login/browser.py) — Updated NixOS native browser journey: automatic magic-link sign-in without a second click, remembered cookie, pending MFA, expired-ceremony refusal and cross-replica revocation across three colocated Marshall instances over verified TLS with a virtual WebAuthn authenticator; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-02-automatic-magic-link.md).

**Gaps:** Hardware authenticators, independent physical hosts, actual inbox delivery and production rollout remain unverified. Native replicas share one NixOS VM and PostgreSQL. JavaScript-capable mail scanners may consume links; the native automatic-submit run uses three colocated replicas in one VM.

**Preferred test work:** Retain native three-replica login/revocation and generated independent-MFA histories; add hardware-authenticator and independent-host acceptance in their required environments. Add a controlled JavaScript-capable link scanner and independent-host browser acceptance in their required environments.

## Read the Control room without administrative mutation authority

Boundary: Signed-in read access is separate from administrator membership plus fresh elevation.

Tested scope: Generated and fixed current-role/elevation/stored-login histories through actual Ash actions, mounted LiveViews and exports; recorded native browser scenario with virtual WebAuthn. Campaign 015 separately checks the packaged standalone browser helper with pinned ty; recorded native execution retains its original source and scope.

This does not inject a database fault or prove an Ash authorizer caller boundary.
- **unit, recorded**: [recently elevated ordinary users may read projections](repo:marshall/test/marshall/accounts/control_room_authority_test.exs) — Ordinary users cannot mutate through domain actions.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-control-room-read-access.md).
- **property, recorded**: [current role and login histories govern Control room reads and audited writes](repo:marshall/test/marshall_web/control_room_history_test.exs) — Generated persisted role and session transitions; real browser/domain guards; target and audit invariants.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-control-room-verification.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [native-control-room-authority](repo:nix/tests/machines/control-room/browser.py) — One packaged serving replica, verified TLS, ordinary browsing, virtual WebAuthn and current stored authority.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-control-room-verification.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [native-control-room-authority](repo:nix/tests/machines/control-room/browser.py) — Current native nine-page admin inventory, ordinary reader access, virtual WebAuthn elevation, forged role-write refusal and unchanged audit/role state, revocation, demotion, mounted login expiry and export404. Real sign-out removes remembered custody before fresh-request refusal; one serving replica.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-09-observability-decoupling.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [native-subsequent-mfa-login](repo:nix/tests/machines/mfa-login/browser.py) — Three colocated serving replicas, virtual WebAuthn, subsequent magic-link and remembered login, actual backend and WebSocket observations, admin-UI mounted/copied-cookie revocation including a no-MFA account.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-mfa-login-verification.md).

No independent-host claim.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-cross-replica-revocation.md).

**Preferred test work:** Populate native drill-down pages and remaining administrative mutation families; retain cross-replica browser session revocation and current-role properties.

## Reset an account and refuse administrative mutations after elevation or role loss

Coverage: **covered**. Layers: identity, authorization, transport, recovery.

Boundary: Current stored browser login, administrator role and fixed passkey elevation retained through account mutation and audit commit; retirement keeps its exclusive admission fence before sorted participants.

Required refusal: Reject stale mounted/domain role, global-session, reset, invitation and retirement mutations after authority loss without target, token, credential, outbox/job, deletion or audit changes.

Tested scope: Five real account action families with independent generated histories and 27 passing three-Marshall schedules; captured packaged-browser reset and five stale-page refusals pass with verified TLS and virtual WebAuthn.

- **property, recorded**: [admin-account-history-oracle](repo:marshall/test/marshall/accounts/admin_account_history_test.exs) — Five generated histories call real actions and compare independent permission/target models and complete refusal inventories.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-admin-account-authority-verification.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [completed revocation refuses fresh mutations on every peer without a gate](repo:marshall/test/marshall/accounts/admin_account_distributed_test.exs) — 27 schedules use three independent BEAMs and real PostgreSQL, audit-write gates, sorted consumers, admission fences and peer-side completion; no native guest assertion.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-admin-account-authority-verification.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [native-admin-account-authority](repo:nix/tests/machines/admin-account/browser.py) — One packaged Marshall with verified TLS, virtual WebAuthn, real same-login lock and second-admin role demotion; target reset and five individual stale-page refusals.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-admin-account-authority-verification.md).

**Gaps:** Hardware authenticators, independent physical hosts, SMTP delivery and production rollout remain separate; full/native captures and previous Control room/retirement claims retain their exact revisions.

**Preferred test work:** Preferred property and native evidence is recorded for the stated scope. Physical authenticator/host and SMTP/provider acceptance requires separately provisioned infrastructure.

## Inspect retained job history and retry a failed background job

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, authorization, transport, recovery.

Boundary: Signed-in bounded redacted projections; current elevated administrator, installation/account/raw-job locks and actor/request-bound retry receipt.

Required refusal: Refuse ordinary, locked, demoted or revoked actors and archived/non-retryable jobs; changed request meaning cannot retry another job.

Tested scope: Independent generated capture/authority histories and ten committed schedules on three independent Marshall BEAMs; captured packaged SMTP/browser journey passed real worker retry, current authority, pruning and login revocation in 65.85 seconds.

- **property, present**: [generated mixed job histories expose only redacted fields to current readers](repo:marshall/test/marshall/administration/jobs_test.exs) — Mixed job states, nested and array raw credential markers and errors retain only approved list/detail/event keys for an ordinary reader; both reads refuse after login revocation.
- **property, recorded**: [retry histories bind receipts while role elevation and login authority change](repo:marshall/test/marshall/administration/job_history_test.exs) — Independent authority, raw eligibility and receipt models through actual domain actions; exact no-change refusals and historical request binding.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-job-history-verification.md).
- **property, recorded**: [generated transitions retain exact redacted history through pruning and expiry](repo:marshall/test/marshall/administration/job_history_test.exs) — Generated fixture raw transitions against actual database capture and ordinary projections; no worker execution claim.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-job-history-verification.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [job-history-three-independent-peers](repo:marshall/test/marshall/administration/job_history_distributed_test.exs) — Three independent BEAMs share committed PostgreSQL; real retry/elevation/sign-out/demotion writes and fixture prune/completion schedules in both orders. Four targeted omission-mutant failures.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-job-history-verification.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [native-audited-job-retry](repo:nix/tests/machines/job-history/browser.py) — Packaged worker real SMTP failure and browser manual retry, virtual WebAuthn, ordinary reads, stale authority refusal, retained pruned history and global login revocation; complete captured journey passed in 65.85 seconds.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-job-history-verification.md).

**Gaps:** Capture overhead, database throughput, independent physical hosts, hardware authenticators, SMTP inbox delivery and production rollout remain outside finite functional evidence. Historical passes retain their captured revisions.

**Preferred test work:** Both preferred forms have recorded execution for this functional scope. Add bounded capture-cost/load measurements before claiming throughput; physical-host and rollout acceptance remain separate.

## Invite, retire and register a fresh identity with the same email

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, authorization, transport, execution, storage, recovery.

Boundary: Verified email identity, current elevated administrator, immediate account revocation and old-UUID cleanup through physical lifecycle fences.

Required refusal: Reject ordinary/self/mismatched retirement, pre-retirement links, copied revoked sessions and inherited credentials or grants; retain disk holds during busy or unavailable execution.

Tested scope: Historical 009 retirement/cleanup and the complete fourth extended NixOS journey on f4de549a retain their email-derived identity scope. Current database tests establish canonical reservations, migration and rollback. Original feature NixOS (484.07 seconds) and Ubuntu (541.14 seconds) generated-identity lifecycles passed retirement closure, fresh same-email/no inheritance, physical cleanup, retained shared GUID/PID, old-home reservation, explicit fresh sharing and packaged Lyra SSH. Those records apply to base 63a1443c plus feature f1bd0e05. Separate incoming d4499c4c merged eight-subtest lifecycle captures passed on NixOS438.33seconds and Ubuntu574.18seconds; they do not establish later browser-launcher/shared-observability integration.

- **machine, recorded**: [fresh same-email registration inherits nothing while old cleanup remains pinned](repo:nix/tests/machines/account-lifecycle/driver.py) — Separate feature f1bd0e05 plus incoming d4499c4c merged captures passed all eight subtests on NixOS438.33seconds and Ubuntu574.18seconds: real retirement, fresh same-email identity/no inheritance, pinned cleanup, retained shared storage/process/home, permanent old-home reservation and explicit fresh re-sharing. One disposable KVM failure domain; not later incoming source or complete Ubuntu SSH/editor acceptance.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-10-durable-guest-identities.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [retirement rejects an active team owner with a stable reason](repo:marshall/test/marshall/accounts/account_lifecycle_test.exs) — Base 63a1443c plus durable feature f1bd0e05, before incoming d4499c4c integration: Current focused lifecycle/Ingress 26-test rerun: a real team-owner refusal after GuestAccount retirement begins preserves the prior account marker/audit, active User and absent deletion receipt through transaction rollback.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-10-durable-guest-identities.md).
- **machine, present**: [lifecycle-refused-admission-retry](repo:nix/tests/machines/account-lifecycle/driver.py) — Present revised disposable fixture requires two real fresh enabled/execution-ready host/pool reports initially and one during the deliberate owned-host outage. It retries only exact closed insufficient_capacity with unchanged request key and bounded deadline; accepted/unknown outcomes stop. Both native witnesses passed creation under real admission, without fabricating telemetry/capacity; the refusal-retry branch is not separately forced..
- **machine, present**: [lifecycle-independent-shared-transport](repo:nix/tests/machines/account-lifecycle/driver.py) — Present corrected fixture resolves co-location through authorized public relocation, waits complete/finished plus running/current destination and requires distinct hosts before GUID/PID capture and owned-host fault. Both complete native witnesses and composed-driver checks passed; no readiness or placement is fabricated..
- **machine, recorded**: [lifecycle-enrolled-transfer-identity](repo:nix/tests/machines/account-lifecycle/driver.py) — Base 63a1443c plus durable feature f1bd0e05, before incoming d4499c4c integration: Both complete native witnesses wait for the actual transfer listener, validate canonical eth2 IPv4 and Ed25519 public key, enroll the endpoint through the real host command and assert exact persisted equality before authorized relocation/setup. - **machine, recorded**: [lifecycle-no-shared-retirement-deletion](repo:nix/tests/machines/account-lifecycle/driver.py) — Base 63a1443c plus durable feature f1bd0e05, before incoming d4499c4c integration: Both complete native witnesses capture whole-VM delete count after completed authorized setup relocation, prove that total unchanged after retirement and separately prove zero retained-current-placement deletes. Retained GUID/PID/native storage/home and fresh-VM checks also passed.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-10-durable-guest-identities.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [lifecycle-stream-lease-closure](repo:nix/tests/machines/account-lifecycle/driver.py) — Base 63a1443c plus durable feature f1bd0e05, before incoming d4499c4c integration: Both complete native witnesses closed already-open invited SSH streams with successful client-unit completion; the wait after browser retirement observation took 0.086 seconds on NixOS and 0.075 seconds on Ubuntu. This is not measured database-commit latency. - **machine, recorded**: [fresh same-email registration inherits nothing while old cleanup remains pinned](repo:nix/tests/machines/account-lifecycle/driver.py) — Base 63a1443c plus durable feature f1bd0e05, before incoming d4499c4c integration: Both complete native witnesses passed fresh same-email registration (NixOS 52.32 seconds; Ubuntu 41.79 seconds): new User UUID has no inherited roles/credentials/grants/membership/machines, receives different canonical login/UID and opens real own SSH/browser home without retired marker while cleanup stays bound to old UUID. Later old-home reservation, explicit sharing and physical cleanup also passed.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-10-durable-guest-identities.md).
- **property, recorded**: [same-email generations cannot inherit retired authority or redirect cleanup](repo:marshall/test/marshall/accounts/account_lifecycle_history_test.exs) — Twelve generated histories of two or three same-email retirements with real Ash actions and synthetic physical outcomes; no native reclamation claim.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-account-lifecycle-verification.md).

Removing canonical sort causes a PostgreSQL deadlock and failed roster. No physical-host claim.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-fresh-guest-identity-verification.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [retired guest home stays reserved and explicit fresh sharing restores usable access](repo:nix/tests/machines/account-lifecycle/driver.py) — Complete fourth NixOS native journey on f4de549a passed original home/UID/sentinel retention, expiry, sudo removal, old-key/unshared/old-name refusal, distinct fresh account and survivor controls; fresh explicit sharing preserves all historical reservations.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-fresh-guest-identity-verification.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [lifecycle-personal-guest-identity](repo:nix/tests/machines/account-lifecycle/browser.py) — Complete fourth NixOS native journey on f4de549a passed old and fresh personal terminal username/UID/home checks on different VMs with separate sessions; shared-session revocation was not exercised.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-fresh-guest-identity-verification.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [fresh same-email team rejoin preserves every surviving guest identity](repo:marshall/test/marshall/accounts/guest_identity_history_test.exs) — Two real retirement/verified-registration generations and explicit team member rejoin preserve owner/teammate boot rosters and every historical allocation; no inherited membership, no member manage and no native home claim.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-fresh-guest-identity-verification.md).

**Gaps:** Both original feature witnesses and incoming d4499c4c merged witnesses passed after correcting setup co-location/transfer enrollment and the shared-delete oracle for legitimate relocation source cleanup. The final oracle preserves unchanged whole-VM count plus zero retained-placement deletes. Retained disks, restore reconciliation and rollout remain separate; mixed old/new serving releases are unsupported. Arbitrary interruption points, independent physical-host failures, hardware authenticators, live SMTP and later incoming integration remain separate.

**Preferred test work:** Retain both serial generated-identity lifecycle results and historical email-derived evidence at their actual scopes. Extend native arbitrary-interruption and independent physical-host schedules; preserve real admission/reservations and live shared-stream/home witnesses. Real SMTP and hardware authenticator acceptance remain distinct.

## Register or approve an SSH key while an administrator retires its owner

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, authorization, recovery.

Boundary: Shared account admission through the outer manual/approval transaction, exclusive retirement and completed old-UUID credential cleanup.

Required refusal: Retirement-first registration leaves key, approval and audit inventories unchanged; stale actors cannot add registrations after cleanup completes.

Tested scope: Generated same-email identity histories and four deterministic schedules on three independent local Marshall BEAMs sharing PostgreSQL; no VM or transport execution.

- **property, recorded**: [same-email histories release credentials only through old-account cleanup](repo:marshall/test/marshall/ssh/credential_retirement_history_test.exs) — Twelve generated histories of two or three fresh same-email identities, manual/approval registration, duplicates, revoke/re-add, pending and completed cleanup and stale actors.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-credential-lifecycle-verification.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [credential-retirement-durable-oracle](repo:marshall/test/marshall/ssh/credential_retirement_distributed_test.exs) — Four deterministic real Ash action schedules across three independently started Marshall BEAMs, exact account-fence waits and committed key/audit/approval/session/cleanup projections; coordinator excluded from node count.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-credential-lifecycle-verification.md).

**Gaps:** Concurrent native browser/SSH registration-versus-retirement delivery and independent physical control-plane hosts remain unverified. Existing native journeys retain their separate nonconcurrent scope.

**Preferred test work:** Generated histories cover cleanup/reuse and committed distributed tests expose exact database ordering. Add a combined native browser/SSH retirement journey for concurrent transport delivery; native tests alone cannot establish this controlled insertion schedule.

## Rename and remove own passkeys and manage SSH keys from account settings

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, authorization, storage.

Boundary: Live stored login and five-minute passkey elevation gate rename/remove through the locked account row; labels are bounded to 80 characters; accounts hold at most ten passkeys and one hundred SSH keys.

Required refusal: Missing, blank or over-length labels, stale or signed-out sessions, unknown credentials, an administrator's last passkey, limit overflow and consumed challenges refuse; every refusal leaves the complete passkey, verification, token, challenge and audit inventory byte-identical.

Tested scope: Generated inventory histories against the real security action, focused domain/LiveView regression tests and a single-VM packaged browser journey with virtual WebAuthn.

- **property, recorded**: [current login, elevation and inventory govern credential self-service](repo:marshall/test/marshall/accounts/credential_history_test.exs) — Generated rename/remove/verify/lock/register/revoke histories for administrator and ordinary accounts against the real action, with an independent model of login liveness, elevation, labels, MFA generation, challenges and token presence; refused steps require byte-identical passkey, verification, token, challenge and audit rows.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-credential-self-service.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [removing the proving credential ends elevation immediately (ADR-0065)](repo:marshall/test/marshall/accounts/security_credential_test.exs) — Domain regressions: proving removal drops mounted elevation, non-last removal keeps MFA and sessions, second-to-last and last ordinary removal with generation bump and full token revocation, label bounds as closed refusals, administrator last-passkey refusal, ten-passkey begin/finish limits with retained-challenge reuse, and failed registration never enabling MFA.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-credential-self-service.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [invalid or unverified credential changes are refused without crashing the view](repo:marshall/test/marshall_web/security_live_test.exs) — Mounted LiveView regressions: rename, exact 80-character acceptance, label refusal copy, unknown credential, locked elevation and administrator last-passkey refusal.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-credential-self-service.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [one hundred owned keys bound registration; removal re-admits it](repo:marshall/test/marshall/ssh/key_test.exs) — Owner-scoped 100-key bound with closed refusal and re-admission after removal.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-credential-self-service.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [native-credential-self-service](repo:nix/tests/machines/credential-self-service/browser.py) — Packaged Marshall over verified TLS with virtual WebAuthn: two-credential enrollment, rename, non-proving removal with surviving session and mounted elevation, proving removal with immediate mounted-page elevation loss, ordinary last-credential removal revoking its own mounted session, administrator last-passkey refusal and post-sign-out stale-view refusal with exact PostgreSQL assertions.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-credential-self-service.md).

**Gaps:** Hardware authenticators, physically independent serving replicas and production rollout remain external acceptance. The native journey uses virtual authenticators inside one VM.

**Preferred test work:** Add hardware-authenticator and independent-host credential acceptance in their required environments.

## Share a VM and revoke a collaborator

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: authorization, execution.

Boundary: Persisted Cedar role governs each action and live capability renewal.

Required refusal: Reject stale grants and prevent authority from surviving revocation.

Tested scope: Composed generated role-by-entry matrix with an independent literal oracle, refusal inventory equality and cross-peer schedules; renewal subsets retain their 016/021/025 scopes.

- **property, recorded**: [every shared role composes terminal, SSH and web entries in one generated history](repo:marshall/test/marshall/access/sharing_matrix_history_test.exs) — All four shareable roles across terminal open/ticket mode/write, SSH open/renew, private web authorize/exchange/open/renew and read inventories, including demotion, revocation, stale leases, closed-token permanence and two-machine churn.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-sharing-matrix.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [share mutation on one peer decides SSH and web entries on two other peers](repo:marshall/test/marshall/access/sharing_matrix_distributed_test.exs) — Three peers with separate Repo pools and Cedar handles sharing committed PostgreSQL; generated cross-peer schedules.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-sharing-matrix.md).
- **property, present**: [persisted role replacement drives the real Cedar permission matrix](repo:marshall/test/marshall/access/sharing_test.exs) — Generated sharing roles against Cedar..
- **machine, present**: [revoking the platform SSH key closes the nested session and preserves the VM](repo:nix/tests/machines/fleet/scenarios/audi.py) — Native live SSH closure and guest survival..
- **property, recorded**: [one generated role history drives terminal, SSH and web entry and renewals](repo:marshall/test/marshall/access/sharing_surfaces_test.exs) — One generated direct-role history drives real terminal ticket/attach, SSH roster/open/renewal and private web login/open/renewal per step against a pure model table; allowed renewals extend the same lease inside five seconds, closed leases never revive, and fresh admission after re-sharing yields a new lease identity.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-sharing-surfaces.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [renewal versus revocation serializes on the VM row](repo:marshall/test/marshall/access/sharing_revocation_distributed_test.exs) — Three independent Marshall BEAMs sharing committed PostgreSQL; pg-observed VM-row waiter chains, five-second TTL bound on a renewal winning before revocation, no extension afterwards, and live-but-denied proof that authority decided.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-sharing-surfaces.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [live shared read-only viewer closes on the authority timer after sharing revocation](repo:marshall/test/marshall/terminal/sharing_revocation_test.exs) — Real Viewer WebSocket lifetime: revocation commits with no synchronous relay dispatch, the idle viewer closes within its one-second recheck margin, the guest session stays alive, old tickets stay refused, and re-sharing admits only through a fresh ticket and relay channel.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-sharing-surfaces.md).

**Gaps:** The composed 054 matrix proves every shared role across terminal, SSH and web entries simultaneously at the application layer, including cross-peer withdrawal. A single native journey driving all three entries in one VM lifetime, team-role matrix cells and physical-failure renewal timing remain unverified.

**Preferred test work:** Retain native revocation journeys and extend the generated matrix when actions or roles change; add a native composed withdrawal journey for physical acceptance.

## Keep Customer machines private and isolated

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: authorization, isolation.

Boundary: Owner-only access, no sharing or web exposure, and denied outbound/lateral traffic.

Required refusal: Reject administrator ownership bypass, stale manager grants and forged guest marks.

Tested scope: Customer authorization properties, generated lifecycle/receipt histories across entry actors, generated local guest network identity tuples and real Firecracker guest network/ingress plus owner CLI lifecycle cases.

- **property, present**: [customer grants cannot be created or confer authority](repo:marshall/test/marshall/access/customer_isolation_test.exs) — Generated roles cannot acquire Customer access..
- **machine, present**: [Customer allows native owner SSH but rejects sharing and web exposure](repo:nix/tests/machines/fleet/scenarios/audi.py) — Real guest owner access and exposure denial..
- **machine, present**: [guest marks cannot bypass same-host, cross-host or host isolation](repo:nix/tests/machines/fleet/scenarios/audi.py) — Forged mark and lateral path denial..
- **property, recorded**: [lifecycle commands across profiles admit only current authority](repo:marshall/test/marshall/ssh/commands_test.exs) — Generated OSS/Customer start/stop/rm histories across owner, manager, revoked member, stranger and administrator authors with complete durable intent inventories: refusals add zero durable rows, acceptances add exactly one operation/request/dispatch, receipts replay only for their accepted actor, changed-kind reuse conflicts.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-customer-controls.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [a refused power event keeps its key usable for a fresh different kind](repo:marshall/test/marshall_web/machines_live_test.exs) — Browser request-key reuse after refusal and changed-kind conflict persistence checks for Customer lifecycle controls, plus member/stranger/elevated-administrator detail redirect refusals.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-customer-controls.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [elevated administrator refuses Customer lifecycle but manages OSS](repo:marshall/test/marshall/access/customer_isolation_test.exs) — Real elevated administrator actor is refused Customer reads and lifecycle with zero new durable rows while successfully managing a control OSS machine.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-customer-controls.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [Customer owner CLI lifecycle replays receipts and recreates the real guest](repo:nix/tests/machines/default-oss/driver.py) — Nine-node fleet owner Porsche CLI stop/start/rm on a real Customer guest with receipt replay, process tree and ZFS death and recreation, retained disk identity, member/stranger and fixture-promoted-administrator CLI refusals with unchanged intent inventories, member browser member-page refusal, and Customer egress denial re-probed after the real CLI restart.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-customer-controls.md).

**Gaps:** Native finite scenarios do not prove all packet encodings, kernel versions or physical network configurations.

**Preferred test work:** Retain generated refusal histories and the native owner CLI lifecycle journey; extend native guest SSH and packet matrices separately.

## Create a machine in the browser or Porsche SSH without choosing a profile

Tested scope: Generated browser draft/default/reload histories and SSH semantic-input retry histories; native ordinary-user creation and profile enforcement journey. Campaign 015 separately checks the packaged standalone browser helper with pinned ty; recorded native execution retains its original source and scope.

- **property, recorded**: [profile drafts survive refresh and reload before duplicate submission](repo:marshall/test/marshall_web/default_profile_history_test.exs) — Independent last-choice model through omitted/explicit profiles, refresh, reload, persisted profile and one durable admission.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-default-oss-verification.md).
- **property, recorded**: [omitted and explicit profiles stay pinned through retry histories](repo:marshall/test/marshall/ssh/commands_test.exs) — Pinned profile and unchanged complete VM history/operation/reservation/receipt identities across same-input retries and changed semantic-input refusals.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-default-oss-verification.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [actual OSS guests reach the live local origin and Customer egress times out](repo:nix/tests/machines/default-oss/driver.py) — Packaged browser/Porsche creation, ordinary persisted users, real boot trust, OSS sharing and DNS/HTTP controls, and Customer sharing/read/registration/egress refusal in real nested Firecracker guests.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-default-oss-verification.md).

**Gaps:** The recorded journey uses ordinary users, NixOS guests and local DNS/HTTP. Unauthorized admission, broader peer/spoofing/IPv6, Brady, owner guest SSH and hostile tool execution retain separate acceptance. Campaign 011 separately verified repaired shared NixOS/Ubuntu trust prerequisites; the seven legacy feature matrices were not rerun.

**Preferred test work:** Add native unauthorized creation and owner guest-SSH cases; retain separate packet/tool and complete legacy feature-matrix acceptance after campaign 011's shared trust fixture repair.

## Create, stop, restart and delete a machine

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: transport, execution, storage.

Boundary: Versioned authenticated control transport, durable generation, owned physical process and GUID-bound drives.

Required refusal: Reject illegal workflow transitions and superseded host session nonces; retain holds while physical absence is unknown. Unavailable source capability refuses recovery without rolling back valid physical crash or host report evidence; confirmed stopped compute releases while disk/address remain retained.

Tested scope: Generated operation histories with an independent durable transition oracle, generated Lilly actor attempt-ordering, supersession and actual actor-kill histories with a durable fake executor, plus native lifecycle and storage cleanup. Direct generated lifecycle audit-retention sweeps check bounded batches and maintenance continuations. Generated Lilly transient exchange prefixes followed by version refusal retain durable intent and fence further ticks. Generated authenticated local HTTP host-session takeovers retain exact epoch growth and permanently refuse all consumed older nonces. A new local generated tail checks one to four late receipts after VM archival and changed replay. Current source2 crash and stale-to-fresh-report SQL regressions passed in the final focused 39-check source/snapshot/editor set; native scope remains separate.

## Resize a running or stopped machine and retry after uncertain progress

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, authorization, transport, execution, storage, recovery.

Boundary: Current management authority, retained draft/request identity, host/VM serialization, immutable physical command and exact target evidence.

Required refusal: Reject foreign/revoked management, stale or changed requests and mismatched evidence; retain holds until physical effects are known. Stored VM source overrides supplied snapshot data. Unavailable source capability after confirmed resize commits target sizes/proof, retains disk/address, releases only confirmed stopped compute and completes a fixed failure without blocking cleanup.

Tested scope: Recorded actual-Ash/PostgreSQL histories across four profiles and grant transitions; recorded strictly typed native browser/OSS NixOS guest journey. Current source2 supplied-snapshot and terminal unavailable-restart SQL regressions passed in the final focused 39-check source/snapshot/editor set; recorded earlier native resize journeys predate this guard correction.

## Clone a machine without sharing its mutable disk

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, authorization, transport, execution, storage, recovery.

Boundary: Snapshot ownership, audit and current requester authority, serialized publication/cancellation, immutable snapshot, source restoration and independently proven cleanup.

Required refusal: Refuse withdrawn manager actions and queued activation, changed request inputs, cancelled retries and cancellation after publication; retain unknown cleanup holds. Retain transfer slots until both endpoint revocation receipts; retain the source snapshot hold, the source exclusion and the prune offer through cleanup uncertainty and past the cleanup deadline; refuse stale recovery over newer source intent. Malformed clone control JSON receives a permanent 400 invalid_message before host state changes. Reject a clone or relocation claim while the host transfer slot is occupied.

Tested scope: Recorded generated actual-action histories and five three-independent-BEAM orderings; existing native copy evidence retains its scope and the new browser cancellation journey records runtime separately. Independent upstream histories and native owned-staging evidence retain their recorded scope. The 2026-09-26 clone matrix adds generated profile/size/image-churn histories with source-row comparators, Ash-pinned OSS/customer retention, power and refusal classifications, and new native clone matrix and failure-permutation checks.

No physical launch claim.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-clone-restart-recovery.md).
- **property, recorded**: [published clones retain cleanup authority through failed restoration and replay](repo:marshall/test/marshall/compute/clone_restoration_history_test.exs) — Real Ash/PostgreSQL rollback-sandbox histories with authenticated duplicate effect receipts and synthetic physical outcomes. Retains failed source restoration/retry, newer intent and complete cleanup predicates. Adds independent stable stopped competitor: queued acceptance, one/two/four activation refusals and immediate retries with exact complete six-resource inventories, activation after verified release and fresh reports, original first-release timestamps and competitor allocation preservation. No native, hosted, independent-process, distinct-endpoint or relocation-competitor claim.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-02-clone-competing-activation.md).

- **machine, present**: [clone-destination-loss-mid-receive](repo:nix/tests/machines/clone-permutations/destination-loss.py) — Destination provisioner crash after its receive-created dataset exists: cancellation retains destination hold, both slots and pending effects while the source restores, then real revoke/discard/prune finish after rejoin; cancelled request refuses retry..
- **machine, present**: [clone-transfer-slot-arbitration](repo:nix/tests/machines/clone-permutations/marshall-restart.py) — Two running sources on distinct hosts cloning concurrently: exactly one wins one-slot-per-host arbitration, the loser fails with a durable retryable admission refusal, and an explicit retry completes it with its own snapshot and destination..
- **machine, present**: [clone-marshall-restart-mid-copy](repo:nix/tests/machines/clone-permutations/marshall-restart.py) — Restarting every Marshall replica after the physical receive began resumes the durable admitted request to exactly one complete, independent, marker-checked copy..
