No concurrent Marshall process or native stream observation.

**Gaps:** The cited native audit does not establish all image profiles and every cross-host failure permutation. On 2026-09-26 a fresh production clone recovered through supported retry and its independent copy relocated in both directions. The original older clone disappeared externally before recovery. Isolated native owned-staging recovery passed; full native reruns and clean production clone acceptance of that repair remain pending. Published upstream recovery retains separate generated and recorded native evidence. The fourth capture passed physical cancellation recovery, then failed automatic owner-clone completion after source disk_not_ready. The fifth stopped on a nonterminal capture_unconfirmed progress label before cancellation; its earlier native error is unknown. The bounded oracle now binds retry labels to offered incomplete effects, valid phases and retained commitments, while requiring the successful exact capture receipt and native GUID before proceeding. The sixth confirmed capture but failed source restart with disk_not_ready before cancellation. The seventh identified pool_observation with incoherent_zfs_observation before cancellation. Deeper finite diagnostics distinguish identity/read/stability failures from bounded accounting exhaustion without changing any guard; exact native causality remains unproven. Complete ordinary single-disk acceptance remains pending; all nine failed captures retain their scopes. Inception validation is excluded. The integrated candidate preserves published 951630ee recovery and adds a bounded private public-property recorder; the eighth instrumented run failed queued activation with source_unavailable before physical capture. Six complete successful reads were privately collected without readiness context. Offline disposable-DB recovery identified the refusal and stale retained telemetry at completion, but cannot prove the exact activation-time conjunct. The fixture waits for actual post-startup fresh receipts and settled operations before one coordinator call. The ninth capture failed initial admission before source creation or that activation wait, with 31 invalid_report execution refusals; private recovery retained three accepted reports with valid host/storage identities and no VM or clone. Rejected report bodies and the validation clock are absent, so their exact failing predicate and overload causality remain unproven. Activation-wait execution and complete cancellation/control acceptance remain pending. Instrumented timing is not performance acceptance. Fixture-only guard-stage and signed-age diagnostics preserve validation and rollback, but await native execution. First rejection per stage/replica provides no host-attributed timeline and cannot retrospectively classify run13. The published staging-identity boundary repair's recorded full native zfs-guest journey and the 1487.16-second prepared-receive cloning-fleet pass retain their separate scopes; its deployment and renewed production acceptance are now recorded in the recovery review. Local generated transfer-slot evidence does not establish independent-process races or physical stream cessation before release. ADR-0149 retires diagnostic ingestion/outbox/presentation; pre-cutover evidence is historical. Replacement native/property acceptance must execute separately.

**Preferred test work:** Diagnose the refused admission reports at their actual guard, then run the bounded capture/cancellation journey and unchanged automatic clone control; extend native profile/interruption coverage independently. Synthetic receipts establish only coordinator behavior. Execute the replacement independent-observability native and property scenarios; historical receipts cannot establish this cutover.

## Move a running workload with one stopped full copy and recover interrupted transfer

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: execution, storage, recovery, transport.

Boundary: Pinned transfer, probe-gated source stop and quiescence, one full stream of the stopped disk, atomic placement handoff and destination readiness before source cleanup (ADR-0155).

Required refusal: Do not run both copies or release storage on failed destination cleanup. Reject a clone or relocation claim while the host transfer slot is occupied.

Tested scope: Incoming ADR-0155 stopped-only evidence records a final 20-subtest native relocation-fleet pass in 4018s, including a since-removed legacy-journal migration subtest (ADR-0159 deleted it with the migration); an earlier stopped-only 19-subtest pass took 3991s after the probe-scheduling fix, including source pressure and destination ENOSPC recovery. This is the incoming cut's execution, not a merged-editor integration rerun. Historical pre-copy complete matrix and separate real-ZFS adoption/generated crash-replay records retain their original scopes. The disposable fleet shares one KVM failure domain.

- **machine, recorded**: [assert claim["state"] == ADOPTION_KILL_STATES[operation]](repo:nix/tests/machines/zfs-transfer/driver.py) — Production received-rootfs adoption against real ZFS recovers native destroy, mount and policy interruption markers; two generated replay cases inspect durable claims and cleanup. This does not boot a transferred guest or establish cross-host relocation.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-provisioning-fastpath.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [cancellation after physical source stop restores data and fresh authority](repo:nix/tests/machines/fleet/scenarios/relocation-failures.py) — Source restoration after confirmed physical stop passes in 207.57 seconds in the complete captured disposable native fleet; data, fresh execution authority and cleanup are inspected. One KVM failure domain, without independent physical-host or retained-state upgrade acceptance.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-09-observability-decoupling.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [private_services_ready(source)](repo:nix/tests/machines/fleet/scenarios/relocation-failures.py) — Actual host-bound Ziggy response and bounded exact Audi-private role/version/ready frame recover after physical source reboot within the original setup timeout; only the exact real public issuer root persists across reboot. Endpoint-restart recovery passes in 381.13 seconds with durable receipt replay, data and cleanup; service unit state alone is insufficient. One KVM failure domain.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-09-observability-decoupling.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [Delete while destination cleanup is unavailable retains its disk and address](repo:nix/tests/machines/fleet/scenarios/relocation-failures.py) — Unavailable destination cleanup retains its physical disk, capacity hold and address until actual cleanup succeeds; the captured native case passes in 206.12 seconds. One KVM failure domain, without physical-host power-loss or production acceptance.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-09-observability-decoupling.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [actual endpoint updates resume an interrupted native transfer](repo:nix/tests/machines/fleet/scenarios/relocation-failures.py) — Real endpoint package updates resume the interrupted physical transfer, preserve guest data and complete cleanup in 336.29 seconds in the captured disposable fleet. One KVM failure domain; this does not establish a packaged retained-state legacy upgrade.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-09-observability-decoupling.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [failed destination readiness retries under a new boot without source rollback](repo:nix/tests/machines/fleet/scenarios/relocation-failures.py) — A destination readiness failure retains the fenced source and retries under a new boot without source rollback; actual readiness, data and cleanup pass in 389.19 seconds. Disposable native fleet within one KVM failure domain.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-09-observability-decoupling.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [physical source pool pressure does not block the stopped copy or co-resident guests](repo:nix/tests/machines/fleet/scenarios/relocation-failures.py) — Incoming stopped-only relocation-fleet recorded source pool pressure in its earlier 19-subtest pass after the probe-scheduling fix, followed by a final 20-subtest pass. Actual source pressure does not gate admission because relocation holds no source headroom; guest identity, unrelated native processes/data and cleanup are inspected. Historical 484.63s pre-copy fallback remains separate; no merged-editor or independent physical-host acceptance.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-10-stopped-only-relocation.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [destination ENOSPC preserves holds and resumes after physical headroom returns](repo:nix/tests/machines/fleet/scenarios/relocation-failures.py) — Physical destination ENOSPC retains partial-receive/resume evidence and source holds, then completes adoption, readiness, data verification and cleanup after headroom recovery in 269.67 seconds. Disposable native fleet within one KVM failure domain; faults, oracles and deadlines are unchanged.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-09-observability-decoupling.md).
- **manual, recorded**: [Acceptance failed: a pre-existing clone retained Overalls' transfer slot.](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-production-clone-relocation.md) — Production relocation refusal with an occupied host slot; database confirms no relocation admitted for the new source. Physical movement remains unverified.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-production-clone-relocation.md).
- **property, present**: [generated clone and relocation claims retain exclusive host slots](repo:marshall/test/marshall/compute/relocation_ownership_test.exs) — Local Ash/PostgreSQL generated clone-versus-relocation claim on either endpoint with one to three retries; paired rollback, exclusive per-host occupancy, single owner role and release/reacquisition. No concurrent Marshall process or native stream observation.

**Gaps:** Historical native results before ADR-0155 exercised retired pre-copy and retain their scope; the incoming stopped-only cut has separate 20-subtest evidence; its synthesized legacy-journal subtest was removed with the migration (ADR-0159), and the reduced scenario has not been rerun natively. No complete retained-state upgrade, independent physical-host failure, merged-editor relocation rerun or production acceptance is established. Production acceptance on 2026-09-25 refused transfer_busy due to an older clone retaining Overalls' slot. The newer NixOS Porsche fleet failed clone relocation on temporarily_unavailable while all Marshall backends briefly lost readiness. Neither physical direction nor opposite-direction concurrency was validated in production. Local generated transfer-slot evidence does not establish independent-process races or physical stream cessation before release; uncovered ownership/receipt interleavings remain.

**Preferred test work:** Retain the complete recorded native matrix with exact public-root custody, real private readiness after reboot and original faults/deadlines. Add generated ownership/receipt sequences for uncovered interleavings and validate independent physical-host and retained-state upgrade boundaries separately.

## Open a personal terminal and reconnect safely

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, authorization, execution.

Boundary: Personal account allocation, boot-bound opening receipt, current creator/read-only attachment authority and PTY ownership.

Required refusal: Reject stale login/session/execution authority, expired/foreign/replayed tickets and writable input after downgrade; do not replay uncertain input. A future output sequence closes the affected viewer channel instead of silently stalling it.

Tested scope: Native fleet terminal scenarios, personal-workspace application tests and generated real WebSock attachment histories with synthetic relay dispatch. Generated local host-stream output-gap histories check per-viewer closure, and mailbox-pressure histories check one stalled viewer shed while other channels advance, without composing Lilly or browser reconnect.

- **property, recorded**: [replay credit histories preserve exact output and drain only after consumption](repo:lilly/test/terminal/channel_history_test.exs) — Five generated local TCP Channel/Stream-callback properties cover replay/live byte and frame exhaustion, exact input/wire receipts, duplicates/gap isolation, invalid consumption, EOF with outstanding credit and old-PID rejection by an actual same-ID successor. Both input-credit families and replay byte exhaustion have fixed witnesses. No Stream authentication, guest hello/PTYS, TLS, native isolation or hosted fault claim.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-29-antithesis-property-audit.md).
- **property, recorded**: [live host channels cede on renewal and replacement across session takeovers](repo:marshall/test/marshall/terminal/host_takeover_history_test.exs) — Three generated two-takeover histories run actual local Bandit/WebSock host channels with real credential/session/epoch and PostgreSQL directory authority. Explicit renewal and successor registration both close the old process; stale nonce renewal and delayed old hello are refused while current successor output works. HTTPS and guest replies are synthetic; no timer deadline, native Lilly, remote peer or partition claim.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-29-antithesis-property-audit.md).
- **property, present**: [generated mailbox pressure sheds only the stalled viewer](repo:marshall/test/marshall/terminal/host_stream_test.exs) — Local HostStream with 60 to 64 queued messages on one stalled task and one to three healthy channels; exact output bound, one slow-viewer signal and channel-close frame, healthy sequences advance after shed. No peer or browser transport.
- **property, recorded**: [a future output sequence closes its channel while a replay is ignored](repo:marshall/test/marshall/terminal/host_stream_test.exs) — Generated future gap values after one valid output and duplicate replay check viewer closure, state removal and channel-close control; local HostStream frame decoding only, without Lilly interruption or browser reattachment.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-29-antithesis-property-audit.md).
- **property, recorded**: [generated mass owner death reclaims exactly the available slots](repo:marshall/test/marshall/terminal/peer_budget_test.exs) — Generated saturation at limits two through eight, partial mass owner death, replacement admission and full reuse; no distributed socket or partition claim.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-29-antithesis-property-audit.md).
- **property, recorded**: [issued tickets compose current roles, explicit sharing and one-use socket lifetimes](repo:marshall/test/marshall/terminal/attachment_history_test.exs) — Generated actual Bandit/WebSock attachment processes compose issued/current direct roles, creator versus explicit sharing, ticket consumption/mode/expiry and foreign-actor refusals. Input, resize and production one-second idle checks traverse all six withdrawal boundaries, then permit explicit fresh attachment. Root-session, browser-auth, injected epoch/time and synthetic relay limitations are recorded; unchanged scoped VM/access/capacity/session inventories are inspected.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-terminal-attachment-verification.md).

## Publish a guest web service and withdraw access

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: authorization, transport, execution.

Boundary: Current publication, TLS identity, renewable request lease and current execution. Gateway catalog decoder refuses TTL above five seconds; ordinary web/SSH grants have their separate thirty-second limit.

Required refusal: Close a live response after access becomes private; expire authority during a control outage. After gateway credential rotation, refuse the old credential and any conflicting certificate installation while accepting the newer current generation of the same service.

Tested scope: Native HTTP/WebSocket access and outage expiration scenarios, generated public/fleet listener route refusals, gateway credential-rotation certificate acknowledgement, Audi DNS publication/refusal histories, and Marshall's classified local ACME proof checks over TCP.

- **property, recorded**: [DNS proof checks retain the negative cause class](repo:marshall/test/marshall/ingress/propagation_test.exs) — Generated proof bytes and local DNS-over-TCP success, absent, malformed, expired, mismatched and transport replies; a second property checks two configured replicas in either order. No hosted fault or deployed DNS claim.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-29-antithesis-property-audit.md).
- **property, recorded**: [UDP and TCP answers follow only the latest accepted snapshot](repo:audi/test/dns_replica_test.exs) — Generated route and proof withdrawals, stale and conflicting revisions, expired publication attempts, independently expired routes and malformed snapshots are checked after every prefix against exact local UDP/TCP A and TXT payloads and authority flags; no live expiry timing, cross-host refresh or deployed delegation.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-29-antithesis-property-audit.md).
- **property, recorded**: [publication, lease and withdrawal histories follow one authority model](repo:marshall/test/marshall/ingress/history_test.exs) — Generated service rotation followed by exact active-certificate reacknowledgement recovers local gateway authority; old credential and conflicting binding remain refused. No native gateway restart.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-29-antithesis-property-audit.md).
- **property, recorded**: [listener routing ignores method, host header and supplied machine credential](repo:marshall/test/marshall_web/fleet_endpoint_test.exs) — Generated reserved API and browser path permutations against both plug chains verify uncached 404, unread public body, no cookie and no redirect; live TLS listeners remain untested.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-29-antithesis-property-audit.md).
- **machine, present**: [changing public access to private closes a live guest response](repo:nix/tests/machines/fleet/scenarios/audi.py) — Live response revocation..
- **machine, present**: [control-plane outage expires live authority independently of DNS answer TTL](repo:nix/tests/machines/fleet/scenarios/audi.py) — Lease expiry independent of cached routing..
- **property, recorded**: [generated delayed certificate jobs never activate an older generation](repo:marshall/test/marshall/ingress/fleet_certificate_test.exs) — Sequential production activation with shuffled stale issued jobs, newer pending/failed/issued generations, exact encrypted-chain/timestamp/audit snapshots and active-job replay. Independent workers, ACME faults, gateway delivery and failed-newer recovery remain open.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-29-antithesis-property-audit.md).
- **unit, present**: [catalog freshness accepts five seconds but refuses longer replies](repo:protocol/test/ingress_catalog_test.exs) — Boundary test source checks 5,000 ms acceptance and 5,001/30,000 ms refusal. No new recorded protocol run or live route-withdrawal result is claimed..

**Gaps:** DNS TTL alone is not authority; cross-host refresh and production DNS delegation, CA behavior and sustained load require separate acceptance. Gateway credential rotation and live route restoration have local database evidence but no native journey.

**Preferred test work:** Keep the native outage/refusal cases; add native gateway credential rotation with route restoration, then expand generated publication/lease event ordering.

## Use private team guest web, remove membership and restore fresh access

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, authorization, transport, execution, isolation.

Boundary: Stored browser login, current direct/team Cedar rights, exact app cookie and execution/policy-bound five-second request-start lease.

Required refusal: Withdrawn membership refuses code exchange, fresh opening and renewal; closed or expired streams cannot revive after re-addition. Customer and foreign authority remain refused.

Tested scope: Generated combined direct/team histories and explicit policy, login, execution, close/expiry boundaries; dedicated packaged browser HTTP/WebSocket team withdrawal journey.

## Recover from a failed guest CLI candidate and resume authorized tool work

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: transport, execution, isolation, recovery.

Boundary: Immutable seed, verified candidate digest, saved executable identity and real current VM executor fence.

Required refusal: Candidate spawn failure or unsuccessful registration restores the predecessor; a stale executor cannot retain authority. No semantic rollback is provided after registration.

Tested scope: Generated actual-supervisor Linux namespace histories with synthetic registration, fixed deadline/boot controls, and a dedicated strict NixOS/Firecracker failure journey.

- **property, recorded**: [generated_failed_candidates_restore_and_allow_fresh_retry](repo:lyra/tests/guest_supervisor.rs) — Actual source-built supervisor in private namespaces/chroot; generated revision/failure/boot states, exact bytes/inode restoration and valid retry. Ready receipts are synthetic.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-guest-cli-recovery.md).
- **property, recorded**: [replacement_waits_for_active_work_before_switching_inode](repo:lyra/tests/guest_supervisor.rs) — Actual supervisor retains installed executable until a synthetic active command completes; no real Marshall or guest command claim.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-guest-cli-recovery.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [with a fresh Marshall fence](repo:nix/tests/machines/guest-cli-recovery/driver.py) — Nine NixOS machines, one real NixOS Firecracker guest, ordinary shared operator, direct candidate injection, actual invalidation/fresh fence/command-result joins and physical deletion.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-guest-cli-recovery.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [shared operator prompt wakes guest and completes model tool round trip](repo:nix/tests/machines/fleet/scenarios/agent.py) — Historical NixOS/Ubuntu successful private periodic replacement and continued fenced tool work, predating the new failure fixture; no failure-recovery claim.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-23-private-guest-cli-updates.md).

**Gaps:** Namespace ready receipts do not establish Marshall authority, TLS or guest isolation. Native candidate injection bypasses artifact delivery. Ubuntu failure recovery, real guest-command drain, power-loss/I/O faults, physical hosts and production rollout remain separate; registered-but-bad semantic rollback is outside the accepted decision.

**Preferred test work:** Extend native recovery to Ubuntu failures and real guest-command drain; retain the recorded NixOS failure and historical two-distribution private successful-update scopes.

## Prompt a shared stopped VM and execute an allowed tool

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: authorization, execution, isolation.

Boundary: Write permission, profile tool allowlist, model claim and boot-bound executor fence.

Required refusal: Reject a model response containing any forbidden tool, reject stale executor authority and a late completion after claim expiry, and refuse changed prompt text under an already used request key without another turn or wake.

Tested scope: Generated capability matrices, SQL-backed stopped-VM prompt replay and model-claim expiry histories, and a deterministic-provider native guest tool round trip. Generated Ziggy poll histories stop after version refusal without starting model work.

- **property, present**: [model poll stops after version refusal](repo:ziggy/test/version_refusal_test.exs) — Generated transient poll prefixes before 426 and repeated post-fence poll messages; no new model task in the local worker. No live Marshall or provider.
- **property, present**: [claimed model work never requeues across the expiry boundary](repo:marshall/test/marshall/agents/model_calls_test.exs) — Generated one to four repeated polls on each side of the ten-minute claim expiry with a database-clock oracle; local Ash/PostgreSQL and synthetic provider response only.
- **property, present**: [stopped VM prompt retries and changed payload retain one turn and wake](repo:marshall/test/marshall/agents/prompt_test.exs) — Generated stopped-VM prompt text, repeated keys and changed payload against local Ash/PostgreSQL state; one turn, event, admission and wake receipts, and agent start operation. Synthetic capacity and no native guest wake..
- **property, recorded**: [any forbidden call rejects a whole generated model response](repo:marshall/test/marshall/agents/capabilities_test.exs) — Generated mixed tool responses.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-23-agent-capabilities.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [shared operator prompt wakes guest and completes model tool round trip](repo:nix/tests/machines/fleet/scenarios/agent.py) — Real stopped guest, Marshall, Ziggy and tool executor with fixture model.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-23-agent-threads-foundation.md).

**Gaps:** Native browser-approved CLI journeys are recorded under the agent-cli scenario; this row retains its generated-matrix, SQL-backed prompt/claim and deterministic-provider scopes. Concurrent same-key prompt and model claims, lost replies and actual provider effects remain open. Native hostile-tool/profile permutations and Ubuntu browser-approved CLI remain open; live external providers remain an excluded boundary. A live Marshall version-skew deployment and already-active provider tasks remain outside the local poll test.

**Preferred test work:** Keep property capability matrices and extend the native CLI approval and denial journeys.

## Use private inference and retain usage receipts through an outage

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: authorization, transport, execution, storage, recovery.

Boundary: Marshall authorizes current guest inference; Ziggy durably reserves bounded usage metadata before provider effects and removes completed receipts only after acknowledgement.

Required refusal: A full spool refuses new provider effects; an incomplete admission cannot be acknowledged as completed usage or replayed as inference after restart. A conflicting completion remains visible as its own class and keeps the disputed receipt.

Tested scope: Generated Ziggy spool saturation, partial acknowledgement, restart, exact capacity reuse and local completion-reply classification; existing native fixture-provider agent journey remains separate. Generated Ziggy version-refusal histories preserve durable completions while stopping automatic reporter retries. Real Reporter controlled initial batches preserve immutable receipts through refusal histories and drain healthy neighbors around one conflict.

- **property, present**: [explicit wrong Ziggy body versions receive permanent refusals](repo:marshall/test/marshall/control/satellite_version_test.exs) — Generated wrong body versions across all closed Ziggy control ops answer unsupported_version, with absent versions retained as invalid_request; local handler only, without hosted rollout.
- **property, present**: [completion reporter stops after version refusal and retains receipts](repo:ziggy/test/version_refusal_test.exs) — Generated transient completions before HTTP 426, multiple retained real spool entries and stopped automatic retries; separate verified local TLS oversized-426 routes. No live Marshall or provider.
- **property, recorded**: [completion replay and conflict histories count each request once](repo:marshall/test/marshall/inference/requests_test.exs) — Generated repeated replay and conflicting completion histories preserve one exact request row and one daily usage increment after every prefix; local PostgreSQL fixtures only.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-29-antithesis-property-audit.md).
- **property, recorded**: [only an exact completion conflict is classified as a durable conflict](repo:ziggy/test/control_response_test.exs) — Generated route, HTTP status and reply-error combinations preserve the distinct 409 completion_conflict class; local response mapping only, without live Marshall or reporter timing.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-29-antithesis-property-audit.md).
- **property, recorded**: [completed receipts consume slots until their exact acknowledgements](repo:ziggy/test/spool_test.exs) — Generated bounded spool saturation, partial exact acknowledgements, process restart and reclaimed slot reuse; local durable files only.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-29-antithesis-property-audit.md).
- **property, recorded**: [mixed reporter histories retain exact receipts and drain healthy neighbors](repo:ziggy/test/reporter_history_test.exs) — The real Reporter/durable Spool history property passed seed203368 in7.6seconds with warnings-as-errors, exit0: fixed2/127/128/129 receipt counts, generated refusal order/repetition, exact retry bodies/bytes, exact per-batch attempt sets, one-conflict neighbor drain and restart totals. Full Ziggy precommit passed64 checks (15 properties,49 tests), seed521050,61.7seconds; forced Dialyzer5.33seconds, zero errors/skips/unnecessary skips, exit0. Controlled callbacks do not establish timer cadence, HTTP/Marshall commit recovery, native faults or full-batch-conflict fairness.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-03-ziggy-reporter-histories.md).

**Gaps:** No generated Marshall acknowledgement outage with reporter drain timing or live provider acceptance is recorded for this property; native independent-host interruption remains unverified. Verified local TLS and injected control replies do not prove hosted version skew or provider recovery.

**Preferred test work:** Add an integrated faulted Marshall acknowledgement and reporter recovery property, then exercise native provider-independent spool restart and retained usage accounting.

## Approve a packaged agent CLI invocation in an ordinary browser and receive real guest work

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, authorization, transport, execution, isolation.

Boundary: Explicit current browser approval binds an in-memory secret digest to one actor and VM for one hour; every API checks current account/write access and exact thread binding, then ordinary model and guest execution fences apply.

Required refusal: GET and digest URLs cannot approve/authenticate; stale sharing or browser login refuses approval. Existing CLI requests refuse withdrawn write access, retired account, deleted VM, expiry, foreign IDs or changed request payload. Browser sign-out alone does not revoke an independently approved CLI session.

Tested scope: Independent generated direct/team authority and invocation histories plus a dedicated native ordinary-browser, packaged-CLI, real stopped guest and deterministic provider journey.

- **property, recorded**: [generated approval sequences keep one session per digest with classified refusals](repo:marshall/test/marshall/agents/agent_revocation_history_test.exs) — Repeated, fresh and owner/member digests after withdrawal; expired rows are removed once and live rows persist across another sweep. SQL-backed fixture only.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-29-antithesis-property-audit.md).
- **property, recorded**: [CLI invocations follow direct and team authority histories](repo:marshall/test/marshall_web/agent_cli_history_test.exs) — Public HTTP/LiveView calls after actual sharing/team actions; full persisted-row refusal inventories, fresh digest approvals, exact retry and binding controls, guaranteed expiry/deletion/retirement boundaries and independent browser logout semantics. Synthetic capacity/execution fixtures are control evidence only.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-agent-cli-verification.md).
- **property, recorded**: [browser sign-out and CLI expiry have separate authority boundaries](repo:marshall/test/marshall_web/agent_cli_history_test.exs) — Expiry, deletion and retirement remain present in every generated and shrunk lifetime permutation. Reproduced duplicate-exhaustion seed 605897 now runs the actual HTTP/LiveView and SQL-backed assertions; synthetic capacity/execution fixtures remain application evidence only.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-cli-history-generator.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [browser GET cannot approve and explicit consent completes packaged CLI guest work](repo:nix/tests/machines/agent-cli-journey/driver.py) — Ordinary member browser sign-in and explicit digest approval; packaged CLI against verified HTTPS; real Marshall/Ziggy/Audi/Lilly/NixOS guest wake, model/tool/final response; child capability refusal and mounted stale approval after owner UI sharing withdrawal. Nine NixOS machines on one physical KVM host; isolated provider.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-agent-cli-verification.md).

**Gaps:** Native attempt two's executor interruption remains unexplained; the passing capture predates the subsequent Underclass package pin. Independent physical hosts, production providers/load/rollout, Ubuntu browser-approved CLI and native hostile-tool/profile permutations remain separate. Inception verification is excluded by user direction.

**Preferred test work:** Follow-up 030 adds composed dispatch histories and a native started-effect fixture. Establish native pre-start observation before claiming that case; preserve the earlier unexplained interruption.

## Inspect uncertain guest work and explicitly retry with current CLI authority

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, authorization, transport, execution, isolation, recovery.

Boundary: Current browser/CLI authority, durable command start, VM placement and executor fence; exact disposable guest process interruption.

Required refusal: Refuse expired/replaced executor results and revoked CLI use; never redispatch an uncertain command or turn an identical request replay into new work.

Tested scope: Composed generated application histories plus a strict native started-effect fixture with real packaged CLI and source-built guest; recorded executions are captured at the staged branch tree.

- **property, recorded**: [executor loss preserves one dispatch and requires authorized explicit retry](repo:marshall/test/marshall/agents/executor_loss_history_test.exs) — Every order covers claimed/started loss, replacement/synthetic expiry, repeated polling, stale transitions, request identity and sharing withdrawal/regrant; complete public-table refusal inventories. Synthetic SQL control evidence only.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-executor-loss-histories.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [real started effect is frozen before targeted executor and child loss](repo:nix/tests/machines/executor-loss/driver.py) — Ordinary browser/packaged CLI, real Standard guest effect, external cgroup observer, exact executor/child fault and actual replacement fence, explicit retry and UI revocation. Pre-start native loss is excluded.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-executor-loss-histories.md).

**Gaps:** Native pre-start loss has no safe identified observation seam; application histories use synthetic running receipts/expiry and model results. The recorded native run is one started-effect interruption on one staged tree. Earlier native CLI attempt two remains unexplained. Physical hosts, Ubuntu, live providers, native expiry histories and long-duration replay absence remain separate.

**Preferred test work:** Retain native pre-start interruption as follow-up until its observation seam is established; rerun the strict driver after agent executor, fence or helper contract changes.

## Fetch through filtered web egress without stale attribution

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: isolation, transport, execution.

Boundary: Kernel flow label, current execution proof, verified origin and fail-closed proxy path.

Required refusal: Do not attribute an old flow to a new owner after address reuse.

Tested scope: Native guest request attribution, restart and address-reuse cases; recorded single-host production NixOS/Ubuntu HTTPS, public cache hits and protected/Customer refusal before and after stop/start.

- **machine, present**: [address reuse cannot attribute an old kernel flow to the new owner](repo:nix/tests/machines/fleet/scenarios/brady.py) — Native stale flow attribution refusal..
- **manual, recorded**: [verified-https-redirect-clone-repeat-protected](repo:scripts/egress_acceptance.py) — Overalls 5f5225f3 before/after guest stop/start and ca739afa after healthy activation: actual NixOS and Ubuntu system trust, signer chain, independent digest, shallow clone, redirects, current resolved owner/VM/generation HTTPS cache hits, protected destinations and independently reachable pinned-IP Customer connection refusal. Later complete stages passed; earlier SSH closures and a failed long-session diagnosis remain unresolved transport gaps. Shared-service faults remain isolated.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-02-https-egress-production.md).

**Gaps:** This does not prove every HTTP/TLS parser input or all cache-policy combinations through real guests. One-host production evidence does not establish relocation, fleet capacity or universal provisioning recovery; the initial Ubuntu create failed on an incoherent ZFS observation and was recovered separately.

**Preferred test work:** Pair NixOS packet-path tests with property tests for cache eligibility and parser bounds.

## Resume a public download after interrupted cache publication or restart

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: transport, storage, recovery.

Boundary: Verified origin and current profile admission precede shared-cache policy; explicit-public or reviewed anonymous full-commit GitHub classification, complete payloads, exact variants, freshness and process-owned leases govern reuse.

Required refusal: Refuse private or conditional requests even when a public range is cached; never publish short, overflowing, aborted or owner-interrupted writes.

Tested scope: Generated real Cache/Store file and process histories, independent full/range bytes, expiry, restart, synthetic pressure and refusal inventories; native HTTP/HTTPS ZFS recovery, real quota/headroom pressure and single-host production shared public HTTPS hits. Final native large matrix covers empty through 256 MiB fixed/chunked bodies, second peer reuse, restart/offline full/ranges, sampled RSS, four concurrent reads plus a slow reader, sensitive/incomplete refusal and reduced native quota. Stale same-mount pressure retains reads while refusing writes until fresh recovery. Overalls real pinned nixpkgs independent-store flakes and a 318,532,502-byte LLVM archive have exact byte/NAR and current attributed cross-guest cache-hit evidence. The 2026-10-03 native extension passed full fixed/chunked delivery through 4 GiB + 1 byte, offline restart/ranges at 2^31/2^32 and four simultaneous 4 GiB readers with unchanged RSS bounds. Baseline live NixOS/Ubuntu acceptance at ef8e997f verified two 7,487,562,572-byte public checkpoint downloads each against independent pre/post SHA-256 references and exact current-owner/generation cache hits. Post-deployment b7e29b8f stable-post-4gib verified a new 7,487,562,572-byte NixOS publication then hit, two Ubuntu hits on that object and matching independent pre/post references.

- **machine, recorded**: [verified HTTPS cache serves full and range bytes after restart with origin stopped](repo:nix/tests/machines/brady-network/storage.py) — ca739afa, driver SHA-256 49d8994f200980218331219d9557f1768803b019d3abb8153769c464f9b5cc20: packaged native TLS/ZFS, separate HTTP/HTTPS publication, complete full/range byte-and-length equality after proxy restart with origin stopped, real pressure/quota cases. Veth peers; no Firecracker trust or authoritative owner claim.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-02-https-egress-production.md).
- **property, recorded**: [public download histories publish only complete current origin and variant bytes](repo:brady/test/brady/cache_history_test.exs) — Real files and owner processes, incomplete publication, abrupt Store restart, exact full/range bytes, all seven origin/variant identities, expiry and pressure stop/resume. Independent payload/freshness model does not use production Policy or Range as its oracle.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-cache-history-verification.md).
- **property, recorded**: [current private requests and responses cannot consume or populate shared objects](repo:brady/test/brady/cache_history_test.exs) — Current case-varied duplicate credential/conditional headers composed with ranges before/after publication and restart; personalized/ambiguous responses and range admission; exact file/accounting refusal inventories.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-cache-history-verification.md).
- **property, recorded**: [reader ownership survives pressure and releases on close or process loss](repo:brady/test/brady/cache_history_test.exs) — Actual foreign-process lease refusal, live-reader retained bytes, unpinned neighbor eviction, close/death release and later reclamation/restart; component pressure observations only.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-cache-history-verification.md).

## Rotate secrets without exposing or losing authority

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, transport, recovery.

Boundary: Private file ownership, global recovery membership, selected host recipients, retained rekey identity, per-document publication, staged compatibility and old-key retention.

Required refusal: Refuse unretained rekey identities, nonrecipient decryption, unsafe custody and incompatible cutovers; preparation failure preserves all originals and partial publication is reported for explicit retry.

Tested scope: Generated local public CLI recipient/recovery-only and interrupted-rekey histories with actual SOPS/age, unpinned generated full recipient turnover through the documented two-generation ceremony with direct-turnover refusal pairs, existing systemd environment properties and historical NixOS custody/coordinated credential rotation.

## Reactivate a historical fleet snapshot safely

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, authorization, execution, storage, recovery.

Boundary: Post-backup revocations, epochs, generations and physical survivors must be reconciled.

Required refusal: Never treat a readable restored database as current production authority.

Tested scope: Current-protection pins only: the isolated restore drill boots the release in operator mode against the restored database with no listener sockets and no background consumers; fixed Lilly tests pin monotonic command receipts under regressed replay, while generated terminal-error histories stop one to four actor processes and preserve durable placements without proving native guest survival; guest-facing identity checks reject journal-only authority after restore. No complete automated historical-fleet reactivation acceptance is identified; the quarantine-gate and default-deny design sketch (restore sentinel, fail-closed quarantine boot, reconcile report, gated finalization) remains proposal-only pending user delegation (candidate ADR-A quarantine gate, ADR-B default-deny re-enablement).

- **property, present**: [terminal exchange fences stop generated actor rosters without losing intent](repo:lilly/test/lilly/control/historical_fence_test.exs) — One to four real actors across six terminal exchange classes; all actor processes stop, every durable Store command remains, status stays unavailable and ticks cannot self-recover. No native VMM, Marshall allocation or external restart claim.
- **machine, recorded**: [restore recovers deleted host bytes and a usable isolated database](repo:nix/tests/machines/backups/driver.py) — Recorded native restore drill: the operator-mode boot against the isolated restored database starts exactly the persistence, encryption and authorization children with no Endpoint process, and Oban normalizes to no queues, plugins, stager or peer leadership, so no AshOban trigger or queue consumer runs. Decryption succeeds only with the restored keyring.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-historical-authority-characterization.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [regressed command replay is acknowledged without rolling back durable intent](repo:lilly/test/lilly/control/historical_fence_test.exs) — Historical fixed fence characterization: regressed and duplicate command replay acknowledge after rereading matching-or-newer durable intent without rollback; unknown-pool and corrupt-store receipts fence :command_store_failed without acknowledging; :unauthorized/stale-session results stop actors, terminal and clone/transfer workers, preserve durable intent and never reopen by themselves. The original capture also included an ambient-host inventory assertion removed in the current merge; it establishes no controlled native inventory or Runtime-init fence composition.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-historical-authority-characterization.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [large tracked unit inventory omits descriptions and still rejects unknown resources](repo:nix/tests/machines/native-zfs/driver.py) — Incoming d4499c4c merged native subtest passed8.22seconds:56 real sleeping tracked systemd units/cgroups exceed the verbose64KiB inventory frame; compact ID-only inventory accepts, omitted live placement and unknown child cgroup refuse. Ownership.verify_units with actual systemd/cgroups only, without Firecracker or Runtime initialization composition. Complete native-zfs later failed unrelated auto-update enrollment receipt validation, so no complete suite pass.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-historical-authority-characterization.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [physical units unknown to the durable command set fence execution readiness](repo:lilly/test/lilly/control/historical_fence_test.exs) — Incoming bee03919 captured separate-BEAM fake systemctl unknown-unit and failed-scan refusal with exact ID-only arguments; avoids assumptions about the developer host and changes no shared PATH. Latest combined execution remains separate, without native cgroup ownership or historical fleet reactivation claim.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-10-browser-launcher.md).
- **unit, recorded**: [a restored journal cannot resolve egress before a fresh running observation](repo:lilly/test/vm/actor_test.exs) — Guest-facing restored-authority refusal: after an actor restart the recovered journal authorizes neither ingress nor egress before the new incarnation records a fresh physical running observation; missing owner metadata and durably superseded intent stay unresolved.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-historical-authority-characterization.md).

**Gaps:** End-to-end historical authority reconciliation is unimplemented/unverified; restore remains isolated. The local actor-roster property does not establish native VMM survival or Marshall hold retention during a fenced Lilly. Runtime initialization composition of native inventory refusal into the host-wide vm_reconciliation_required fence remains source-inspection evidence. Real unit/cgroup inventory helper passed8.22seconds in the incoming d4499c4c merged native-zfs run, whose complete execution failed unrelated auto-update enrollment-receipt validation; no complete startup-fence or suite acceptance. The removed ambient-host assertion created no controlled unknown unit or failed scan and supplies no physical refusal evidence. Blocked pending a user-delegated architectural decision (new ADR) for quarantine, fencing and reconciliation of post-backup revocations, epochs, generations, reservations, terminal/login grants and pending commands against surviving physical state, with unknown allocations held unavailable.

**Preferred test work:** Build a NixOS multi-host restore scenario and a property model of post-backup revocations before enabling automated reactivation.

## Admit work without double-spending capacity

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: execution, storage.

Boundary: Fresh measured capacity, atomic reservations and owned physical allocation.

Required refusal: Reject stale observations and prevent physical usage from being hidden by logical budgets.

Tested scope: Generated multi-node admission histories against a committed raw-SQL accounting oracle, local insert-failure histories with exact committed row inventory, generated archived same-key replay and changed-shape histories with complete allocation inventory, generated storage-accounting and host report ordering cases, generated authenticated poison-command exchange rollback and owner-stop recovery, local Lilly transient-failure/status recovery histories, and native fleet capacity admission races. A separately captured 2026-10-13 KVM ZFS I/O run kept measured capacity available when independent pool rates were unavailable under malformed, timeout and export/import faults.

- **property, recorded**: [lost post-commit admission replies replay across three nodes](repo:marshall/test/marshall/compute/distributed_admission_test.exs) — Recorded generated committed worker or owned fourth-BEAM loss before response release, exact worker or abrupt137 exit observation, live/archived witnesses and nine concurrent same-key retries across three surviving BEAM nodes per round; complete row inventory and changed-shape refusal. No HTTP response loss or native VM execution evidence.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-05-admission-lost-reply.md).
- **property, present**: [archived admission replays retain the complete allocation inventory](repo:marshall/test/marshall/compute/admission_test.exs) — Generated one to four same-key retries after VM archival, followed by changed-shape refusal, preserve VM history, versions, placements, reservations, addresses, operations and dispatch jobs on local PostgreSQL. No lost HTTP reply or cross-node race.
- **property, present**: [transient exchange failures back off and a delivered report restores readiness](repo:lilly/test/lilly/control/historical_fence_test.exs) — Generated one to six classified transient failures at the Lilly Runtime handler, bounded retry delay, unchanged local Store and status refusal, then injected successful report exchange and ready status in the same session; no live transport or Marshall freshness probe.
- **property, present**: [a poisoned command rolls back fresh reports until its operation is removed](repo:marshall/test/marshall_web/provisioner_api_test.exs) — Generated one to four authenticated exchange retries with a durable invalid command snapshot, optional acknowledgement and valid report. Stale host state persists until a real owner stop cancels the ordinary operation; the next exchange restores freshness and delivers the stop command.
- **property, recorded**: [injected insert failures preserve the admission commit set](repo:marshall/test/marshall/compute/distributed_admission_test.exs) — Both CPU shapes and shuffled insert failures at twelve local stages; complete public-table equality after refusal and linked acceptance, replay and conflict inventory checks on one Marshall node.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-29-antithesis-property-audit.md).
- **property, recorded**: [generated report histories preserve epoch order and atomic rejection](repo:marshall/test/marshall/compute/host_reports_test.exs) — Real Ash/PostgreSQL host and pool rows across generated accepted, duplicate, older, old-epoch, partial and reconnect actions; fixture telemetry only.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-29-antithesis-property-audit.md).
- **property, present**: [physical usage gates admission independently of legacy logical budgets](repo:marshall/test/marshall/compute/budget_test.exs) — Generated usage/budget values..
- **property, recorded**: [race result validation rejects raw and Ash-wrapped clone SQL failures](repo:marshall/test/marshall/compute/distributed_capacity_race_test.exs) — Actual returned-result validator rejects nested structured Postgrex deadlocks and opaque clone errors; documented atom refusals and successes remain valid. The focused real three-node race and wrapper suite passed two properties, seed202304,60.2seconds, warnings-as-errors, exit0. No new lock schedules or universal opaque-error detection. Full Marshall precommit passed 1,423 checks (222 properties, 1,201 tests), two exclusions, seed59328,1465.7seconds; forced Dialyzer44.48seconds, zero errors/skips/unnecessary skips, exit0.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-03-distributed-race-error-oracles.md).
- **property, recorded**: [opposite-source relocations and clone activation reach shared host locks](repo:marshall/test/marshall/compute/distributed_capacity_race_test.exs) — Three independent Marshall BEAM peers; controlled PostgreSQL holder and tagged blocking chains, including a direct transaction-ID wait; exact committed endpoints, real activation, accounting and fixture restoration. Focused property passed seed219491 in51.6seconds, exit0. No native transfer, hosted search or order mutant evidence.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-03-distributed-host-lock-reach.md).
- **property, recorded**: [generated multi-node races preserve committed capacity accounting](repo:marshall/test/marshall/compute/distributed_capacity_race_test.exs) — Generated operation histories over three independent Marshall BEAMs with synthetic-but-committed host telemetry.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-27-capacity-races.md).
- **machine, present**: [capacity races spend pending startup headroom only once](repo:nix/tests/machines/fleet/scenarios/capacity.py) — Native fixed and generated fleet admission races under capacity pressure..
- **unit, recorded**: [readiness observation reports identity refusal without raw paths or changed results](repo:lilly/test/host/zfs_logging_test.exs) — Frozen diagnostic-only missing-path regression proves unchanged incoherent refusal, fixed canonical_path/before metadata, private raw path/dataset and silence for ordinary or invalid-UUID calls. Focused 21 checks and full Lilly/Observability gates passed; no parent-accounting, healthy-backing, native storage or retry-success proof.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-10-browser-launcher.md).

No physical IOPS, production host, composed-driver type check or fleet admission claim.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-10-13-antithesis-prerequisites.md).

**Gaps:** Local insert faults and recorded post-commit worker/BEAM loss with concurrent three-node retries do not cover pre-commit ambiguity, HTTP reply loss, optional workspace-source or Inception environment writes, or native job execution. The poison-command test does not cover Lilly's response to that durable 409, retained relocation-cleanup obligations or a policy decision on whole-host report starvation. The Lilly transient property injects handler results and does not run client retry, a real partition or Marshall freshness recovery. These checks do not establish fleet-wide production throughput or arbitrary overcommit tuning.

**Preferred test work:** Keep native admission races and generated accounting properties; add a cross-service partition/heal journey through Lilly retry and Marshall report freshness, native commit-ambiguity and optional-write failure journeys, test Lilly poison-exchange backoff and retained cleanup recovery, then measure load separately.

## Transfer team ownership without losing its authority invariant

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, authorization, transport.

Boundary: Current team role, durable transfer workflow and exactly one active owner.

Required refusal: Reject owner replacement, unauthorized or terminal transfer transitions and stale authority after transfer/removal.

Tested scope: Generated PostgreSQL role/consent histories and a three-account NixOS Chromium journey over verified HTTPS. SSH HTTP properties and packaged CLI assertions inspect permanent refusals and pinned historical receipts. Generated actual resource actions, four full account-retirement schedules across three independent Marshall BEAMs, and recorded certified guest SSH/private integration membership transitions extend current-authority coverage. Campaign 015 separately checks the packaged standalone browser helper with pinned ty; recorded native execution retains its original source and scope.

- **unit, present**: [database rejects direct mutations that leave an active team without an owner](repo:marshall/test/marshall/teams_test.exs) — Database constraint and domain authority tests..
- **property, recorded**: [generated membership and consent histories preserve current authority](repo:marshall/test/marshall/teams_history_test.exs) — 40 generated histories of actual domain operations; fresh reads, terminal immutability and deferred owner constraints inside SQL sandbox.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-team-authority-verification.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [ownership consent and membership revocation reject stale browser authority](repo:nix/tests/machines/teams/default.nix) — Real release/PostgreSQL, three browser accounts checked ordinary after login and operations, verified HTTPS, committed transfer/removal and stale-page refusal.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-team-authority-verification.md).

## Deploy a candidate while preserving guests and authority

Coverage: **partial**. Layers: identity, execution, recovery.

- **property, recorded**: [def test_operation_histories(](repo:nix/tests/machines/auto-update/compatibility_histories.py) — Independent operation-intent oracle for additions, inactive reorder, destructive/shared-authority refusals, sixteen-key bound and corrected retry through actual compatibility and preflight, with unchanged local fixture inventories.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-updater-compatibility-histories.md).
- **property, recorded**: [def test_all_serving_and_inactive_baselines(](repo:nix/tests/machines/auto-update/compatibility_histories.py) — Every serving baseline can veto, an incompatible installed file is ignored only while serving, and actual staged candidate selection bypasses the poisoned live file; environment/discovery seams do not establish native activation.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-updater-compatibility-histories.md).
- **machine, present**: [fixed candidate switches and records verified success](repo:nix/tests/machines/auto-update/driver.py) — Actual system generations and update failure phases..
- **machine, present**: [actual Lilly release upgrades preserve running guests and authenticated control](repo:nix/tests/machines/fleet/scenarios/updates.py) — Fleet guest survival across release and credential updates..
- **property, recorded**: [def test_refusal_backoff_then_manual_verify_only_recovery(](repo:nix/tests/machines/auto-update/custody_histories.py) — Generated refusal cadences (1..22 refusals): exact coordinated_secret_change at preflight through real staged resolution, deterministic backoff doubling to the fifteen-minute/sixteen-failure cap with no expiry, operator manual switch, fetch-only stale window, then verify-only recovery that never re-stages, migrates or switches; published bundle bytes pinned throughout.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-custody-updater.md).
- **property, recorded**: [def test_recipient_only_rekey_descriptor_handoff(](repo:nix/tests/machines/auto-update/custody_histories.py) — Recipient-only rekey through real stage/resolve/descriptor_id handoff with actual ssh-to-age/sops/secretspec: staged bytes published byte-exactly despite a concurrent document replacement at switch time, stale handoff retired at staging entry, handoff keys bound to exact descriptor digests.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-custody-updater.md).
- **property, recorded**: [def test_custody_wedge_label_backoff_and_recovery(](repo:nix/tests/machines/auto-update/custody_histories.py) — Host removed from a still-selected document wedges staging with exact operation_failed/stage label, deterministic backoff, untouched last bundle and uninterrupted serving; both config-removal and custody-restored fixes converge healthy in one tick.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-custody-updater.md).

**Gaps:** Generated local histories do not execute native systemd units, real database migrations, guest-impact activation or live environment replacement on machines; independent physical rollout scheduling remains outside host-local updates and automatic database rollback is forbidden.

**Preferred test work:** Retain native candidate/failure and credential-rotation scopes; extend native live-environment replacement and concurrent delivery evidence separately from the recorded compatibility and custody histories.

## Authenticate CLI installation before adding SSH trust

- **property, recorded**: [generated_denials_dominate_matching_ca_trust](repo:lyra/src/trust.rs) — Reordered revocation and conflicting managed authority never produce replacement trust.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-unified-ssh-host-ca.md).
- **machine, recorded**: [conflicting and revoked trust refuse before replacing installed state](repo:nix/tests/machines/installer-trust/driver.py) — Real HTTPS delivery and Porsche account proof, repeated CA setup and preservation of a distinct prior executable; no guest.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-25-unified-ssh-host-ca.md).
- **property, recorded**: [generated_configurations_preserve_unmanaged_bytes](repo:lyra/src/ssh_config.rs) — Generated pre-existing local SSH configuration combinations (empty, unrelated blocks, prior managed block, adversarial near-marker content); byte-level directory inventory oracle with exactly one marker pair and byte-identical convergence; not a native macOS claim.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-installer-ssh-config.md).
- **property, recorded**: [generated_marker_ambiguity_is_refused_and_preserves_every_byte](repo:lyra/src/ssh_config.rs) — Generated duplicated, reversed and unpaired managed markers refuse with the whole SSH directory byte-identical; symlinked and directory-shaped configs refuse before any write; a dropped named temporary file leaves no residue.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-installer-ssh-config.md).
- **property, recorded**: [generated_known_hosts_configurations_preserve_unrelated_bytes](repo:lyra/src/trust.rs) — Generated pre-existing known-hosts combinations keep every unrelated byte in position, converge byte-identically on reinstall, and refuse ambiguous managed markers and separator-promoted dangling marker text.; [execution record](repo:docs/architecture/reviews/2026-09-26-installer-ssh-config.md).

**Gaps:** Native macOS installation/runtime and arbitrary pre-existing SSH configurations remain outside this tested Linux scope; guest connections are recorded under ssh-host-ca and the fleet user journey revocation cycle.

**Preferred test work:** Preferred native and generated refusal coverage is recorded; add native macOS execution and generated pre-existing SSH configuration combinations.
